## Session 8 addendum (2026-06-01) — #11 Phase 1 EXECUTED (the close-out track resumes)

<!-- D/C/F: D-140 -->
**D-140 (#11 Phase 1 EXECUTED — `divmul_pow10` proven-exact + D-100 oracle built; resolves synthesis H1).** Session 8 (2026-06-01, fresh `/accept-handoff` pickup) ran the close-out track's Phase 1 — the #1 ship risk (a deterministic-but-WRONG money core). Artifacts: `plan_checks/2026-06-01-11-phase1-divmul-proof/{divmul_pow10_proof.py, decimal_oracle.py, PROOF.md}` (stdlib-only, reproducible).
- **`divmul_pow10` PROVEN-EXACT.** The decimal `÷10⁸` reduce lowers to `floor((P·M) >> S)` with constexpr `(M,S)`, bit-exact `== floor(P/10⁸)` over the proven dividend range. Proof = analytic Granlund–Montgomery bound + the exactness predicate VALIDATED against fully-exhaustive brute force (170 `(d,N)` pairs; rejects too-small-S; no false-positive) + a 208k differential (binding-set `{d·k−1,d·k,d·k+1}` + powers-of-ten + boundaries + 200k random) vs exact-`//` AND Python `decimal`. Replaces the `__udivti3` 128-bit libcall (~40–100 **variable** cyc) with a fixed ~20-cyc multiply+shift — the **determinism** win (kills the latency jitter) over the raw-speed win. Resolves synthesis **H1**: "hot path UNTOUCHED" now stands for the reduce.
- **Locked constants — N=127 RECOMMENDED:** `M = 0x55e63b88c230e77e7ee106959b5d3e1f`, `S = 153`; 254-bit product **fits #2's existing 256-bit wide-multiply** (reuses the 128×128→256 product, grabs the high end `>> S`). Alternatives: N=120 conservative (241-bit, 15-bit margin); N=128 full-`__int128` REJECTED (257-bit product, 1 over 256). **Overflow guard** `|operand| < 2⁶³` (⇒ `P < 2¹²⁷`) makes it correct-by-CONSTRUCTION — out-of-range routes to the D-106 range-guard/flag, NEVER a silent wrong-magic. Venue `P_max ≈ 2¹¹⁰` (Binance maxPrice×maxQty, D-106 venue-as-SSoT) ⇒ ~17 bits headroom. N=127 chosen = widest proven range that fits the existing primitive (least discipline-dependency); both N proven ⇒ refinement, not fork.
- **D-100 oracle BUILT + CLEAN.** `decimal_oracle.py` = INDEPENDENT exact-`Decimal` reference (golden-master per `feedback_golden_master_over_reimplemented_oracle`, NOT a reimplementation — value comes from `Decimal`) for the step-5 op-set #2–#6; the engine's branchless lowerings match it (300k signed muls, 1k half-even ties at `2r==SCALE`, 50k exact parses, malformed/>8dp/scientific-notation reject, 50k quantize). Becomes the ship's standing acceptance gate (grows a recorded/Binance-testnet-fill differential at Ship A/B). The harness self-caught a venue-contract asymmetry (`Decimal` accepts `"1e5"`, the venue never emits it) → oracle models the venue alphabet `[+-0-9.]`.
- **Feeds:** the #3 design (drop-in constexpr M/S + `(q,r)` for #4); the ship acceptance gate (the oracle). The general primitive (divide-by-invariant-constant for wide types) = task-#14 DESIGN_SPEC; the proof verifier = CI-gate candidate (→ `tools/`, sister task #26).
<!-- STATUS: decided/proven; resolves synthesis H1; feeds #3 design + ship acceptance gate; PROOF.md = the record; step-6 fold carries N=127 + the guard -->

**D-147 (INT_MIN abs/negate guard for Ship A = branchless saturate-to-MAX in unsigned space; flag-loud deferred to Ship B).** The plan acceptance left the −2⁶³ (`INT128_MIN`) guard as "(saturate/flag)"; RESOLVED for Ship A = **saturate-to-MAX (2¹²⁷−1)** because (a) it matches the existing `of_mask` saturate-on-overflow in `fp2_mul`/`fp2_addsat` that the R2 63-bit-bound safety rests on, and (b) for the binary FEATURE domain −2⁶³ is unreachable (R2: features reach +2⁶³ only near a ~$30M price), so the guard is pure UBSan-hygiene and graceful-saturate beats fail-loud on an impossible value. MUST be branchless + zero-variable-latency (operator: "zero variable latency") AND UB-free: compute the magnitude in `unsigned __int128` (modular, no signed-overflow UB), branchless-clamp to MAX, cast back — verified by the `build.sh ubsan` lane (±INT_MIN probe). `fp2_neg`/`fp2_abs` (`FixedPointN.hpp:1352-1353`, currently bare `-a.v` tagged "INT_MIN guard in prod fold") get the guard AT the flip-fold. Flag-loud (D-106 out-of-range) is the Ship-B money posture (fail-loud where capital lives), NOT the binary core. Sister: D-145 (branchless), R2 (saturation-preserved), feedback_heavier_default_audit_posture_for_capital. **Re-fire precision (independent challenge):** the 16B ceiling 2¹²⁷−1 ≠ 24B sign-mag's ~2¹²⁸ all-ones magnitude — SAME branchless saturate DISCIPLINE, DIFFERENT ceiling, already absorbed by R2's bounded |value|<2⁶³ (no in-range value changes; slice 423/0 proves it). The −2⁶³ unreachability covers the slope/spread NEGATIVE features (`DivNoAssert` ratio-bounded — `RollingStats.hpp:390`/`RegimeDetector.hpp:529`) too, not only the +2⁶³ variance accumulator. Alt `__builtin_{mul,sub}_overflow` REJECTED — UB-free but introduces a data-dependent value vs D-145 zero-variable-latency.
<!-- STATUS: decided; saturate-to-MAX branchless/unsigned-space for Ship A; flag-loud → Ship B; UBSan-verified at the fold -->

Diagnosed: the underlying `rg` invocations are FAST (67/71/256 hits in <8s) — the hang is in the awk wrapper (`struct_map`/`byte_context`), NOT the search. So the tool is fixable (task #3 / D-134 gen_code_map quality), but for THIS flip the R1 enumeration uses the working alternates: (a) a direct `rg "static_assert\(\s*(sizeof|offsetof)"` sweep over the source dirs — which surfaces the "hidden" FPN-CONTAINING struct asserts too (`Position<64>==192`, `Order<64>==320`, FlowFeatures clusters) that a textual `.*FPN` grep MISSES = ~40 asserts across ~10 files (saved at `build_probe/`); (b) the COMPILER RED-BUILD as the authoritative enumeration (every break = a compile error); (c) `tools/check_plan_enumeration_completeness.py` as the AR-1 drop-guard. <!-- D/C/F: D-151 -->
**D-151 (Ship-A keystone mechanism = FULL-SPECIALIZATION, not the literal alias D-143 stated — an alias breaks FPN<F> deduction).** Implementing the flip (Session 10), D-143's "FPN<64> will ALIAS FixedPoint<2,64>" proved UNWORKABLE as a literal alias: `template<unsigned F> using FPN = FixedPoint<2,F>` makes `FPN<F>` a NON-DEDUCED context, so every `FPN_Op(args)` call that deduces F from its argument (pervasive in the F-templated engine) fails — the red-build caught it immediately at `CfgFieldDispatch.hpp:194` (`FPN_ToDouble(src)` couldn't deduce F). MECHANISM ADOPTED: `FPN<64>` is a CONCRETE full-specialization `template<> struct FPN<64> { __int128 v; static constexpr unsigned F=64; }` (a real FPN<F> specialization → trivially deducible), layout-identical to `FixedPoint<2,64>`; `is_fp_binary` is EXTENDED (`template<> struct is_fp_binary<FPN<64>> : true_type`) so the B6 dispatchers read it as binary; `is_FPN_v` unifies onto `is_fp_binary_v`; the op specializations hop through `.v` to the proven `fp2_*`. Same END-STATE D-143 intended (FPN<64>=16B, B6-clean, the ~2,144 sites ride unchanged), but robustly deducible. The generic 24B body is SHED (only <64> defined; the F=128 trait-test is rewritten this ship). Verified by header syntax-check (deduced calls + traits compile); mechanism documented in-code at `FixedPointN.hpp`. Sister: D-143, the B6 disjoint-trait guard, feedback_design_once_maintain_forever (got the mechanism right via the red-build before building dozens of sites on the wrong one).
<!-- STATUS: decided/executed; full-specialization (not alias) — deduction-safe + B6-clean; D-143 mechanism-refined -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-152 -->
**D-152 (Ship-A storage-flip PORTING learnings — tracked for the postmortem + anti-pattern codification at close).** The ~62-site sign-magnitude→two's-complement consumer port surfaced 3 patterns worth NOT reintroducing:
1. **DON'T give the 16B `FixedPoint<2,64>` a sign-magnitude `.w[]`/`.sign` COMPAT interface to avoid porting.** The raw two's-complement words are NOT the sign-magnitude MAGNITUDE words the old sites assume → it would compile-but-compute-WRONG (silent value bug), or require recomputing `|v|`+words per access (slow; defeats the whole point of 16B). The red-build FORCING a per-site port is the safety feature — each port re-examines the semantics (which is how the −0 edge below was caught). **Strongest candidate RECURRING_BUG_PATTERN (codify at close).**
2. **−0 / sign-of-zero is a 24B representation artifact that does NOT carry to 16B.** Sign-mag `.sign` is 1 for negative OR −0; two's-comp `v<0` excludes −0 (none exists). So a sign-check port (`!x.sign`→`x.v>0`, `x.sign`→`x.v<0`) drops the −0 case. VALUE-PRESERVING (−0 == +0 == the value 0; D-139 is about VALUES), but a downstream behavior reading the sign-of-zero (e.g. `PortfolioController` win/loss COUNT) differs; two's-comp is arguably more correct. Flag + accept per-site; not a value-equivalence break.
3. **A magnitude-compare → native `.v` compare is value-equivalent ONLY for non-negative values.** The OrderGates `BuyGate`/`PositionExitGate` 2-word magnitude compares collapse to a single native `price.v >= tp.v` BECAUSE crypto price/volume are always ≥0 and ≪2¹²⁷ (the positivity domain invariant the old code already relied on, "skip sign machinery"). For a possibly-negative value the native signed compare would differ — preserve that invariant.
Each port stays VALUE-EQUIVALENT + branchless (the `.w[]` blends/masks → whole-`.v` blends/masks; the word-loop compares → native `.v`); subtle ones diff-tested (branchless `FromString` 297/0). Backstop = `controller_test` value assertions + the golden re-freeze. Sister: D-139, D-151, [[feedback_golden_master_over_reimplemented_oracle]], RECURRING_BUG_PATTERNS (codify #1 at close).
<!-- STATUS: tracked; porting learnings for the Ship-A postmortem + RECURRING_BUG_PATTERNS codification at close -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-153 -->
**D-153 (Ship-A flip META-HARVEST — DESIGN_SPEC + meta-pattern candidates; slated for ship-close authoring per pattern-codification-lifecycle).** Beyond the porting anti-patterns (D-152), the flip is producing reusable patterns. Slate (author at CLOSE, when the canonical code is complete + proven — NOT mid-flip, premature):
- **NEW DESIGN_SPEC `value-equivalent-storage-representation-flip-methodology`** (meta-disciplines): the safe way to change a foundational type's storage representation — keystone (atomic type redefinition + op-rewire to PROVEN bodies) → compiler RED-BUILD as the authoritative blast-radius enumeration → per-site VALUE-EQUIVALENT port (re-examine semantics each site, never a find-replace) → DIFFERENTIAL-TEST the subtle rewrites → re-freeze the golden. First canonical = this Ship-A 24B→16B flip. Sister: D-135 (red-build-as-enumerator), golden-master, two-foundations.
- **EXTEND `golden-master-over-reimplemented-oracle`** with the differential-test-gates-a-rewrite instance (branchless `FromString` candidate diffed vs the proven digit-by-digit reference; swap only on 297/0).
- **NEW RECURRING_BUG_PATTERN class** — "compat-interface-that-silently-mismatches-semantics" (the don't-fake-`.w[]`-on-the-16B-type trap, D-152 #1; the red-build forcing per-site ports is the structural guard).
- **Meta-refinement of D-139** — value-equivalence is about VALUES, not representation bits; representation-specific behaviors (sign-of-zero) do not carry across a representation change (D-152 #2).
- **Branchless-discipline refinement (sister H7/H20)** — "branchless-where-it-counts": data-dependent DISPATCH branches die (→ masks); a safety-required loop (e.g. null-bounded scan to avoid an untrusted-input over-read) STAYS — its loop-exit is safe-termination, NOT dispatch (`FromString` instance).
- **NEW DESIGN_SPEC `branchless-guard-via-safe-compute-and-mask`** (refactor-patterns; operator: "probably should codify that"): when a guarded computation is SAFE to run unconditionally (e.g. a saturating `FPN_DivNoAssert`-by-zero), compute it ALWAYS then MASK the result to the guard's default — replaces `if (valid) compute else default`, killing the data-dependent branch (H20; a mispredict's variance cascades through dependents). Instances: RegimeDetector `ema_sma_spread` divide-guard (+ the compute-guards surfacing through the port). Sister: branchless-dispatch-discipline (Class 28), `FPN_BlendOnMask`.
- **Memory LANDED this session** — [[feedback_deferral_reasons_merit_not_effort_or_context]] (the AI-effort-isn't-a-deferral-axis meta-collaboration lesson).
<!-- STATUS: slated; author the specs/classes at the Ship-A close postmortem (pattern-codification-lifecycle — canonical code must be complete + proven first) -->

**D-173 (BNB/commission-asset handling LOCKED = the commission-asset CONVERTIBILITY RULE + two-layer guard; the S-1 sub-decision).** Operator converged 2026-06-09 ("sounds good, trusting you some") after due-diligence Q&A; both external claims VERIFIED against Binance docs same-session. **The rule (venue-agnostic, reusable):** book a commission only in an asset convertible to quote EXACTLY via a VENUE-GIVEN number — domain at Ship B = {quote → identity, base → × fill_price (the venue's own number for that transaction)}; anything outside the domain is REFUSED LOUD, never mis-booked. Ticker-conversion REJECTED (no venue-given rate exists for BNB commissions → any rate is an internalized convention, the D-106 anti-pattern); per-asset ledger REJECTED (smears the same arbitrary conversion to read-time across every consumer; premature multi-asset dimension per the D-99 boundary). **Two-layer guard:** (1) BOOT — query the venue's ACTUAL spot BNB-burn state via `GET /sapi/v1/bnbBurn` (endpoint verified; the engine cfg `pay_fees_in_bnb` stays a fee-MODEL knob for paper/backtest — the venue account setting, not our cfg, decides what asset is charged) → LIVE + spotBNBBurn=true ⇒ refuse to boot with instructions; (2) RUNTIME (authoritative — the trade has already executed when commission arrives, so this can only choose HOW to degrade): unconvertible `N` on a fill ⇒ book the COMPUTED fee as a FLAGGED fallback (sticky FailureModeRegistry flag; documented degrade mode, NOT a Class-18 parallel path) + halt NEW entries while exits keep managing. **Verified venue facts:** insufficient/unavailable BNB (incl. staked) ⇒ Binance silently falls back to charging the FULL undiscounted commission in the RECEIVED asset → commission asset varies FILL-TO-FILL (confirms runtime-guard-over-cfg-flag). **Per-fill conversion pinned:** multi-fill accumulation converts EACH fill's commission at THAT fill's price, then sums (never aggregate-then-convert with an average). **Maker/taker independence:** the asset question is rate-independent; engine is MARKET-only (taker) today with the OrderManager fee-desync FATAL tripwire (TECH_DEBT-154 owns maker re-resolution pre-LIMIT); under B1, LIVE booking becomes maker/taker-agnostic (venue reports the actual charge) — maker rates matter only for the computed model. **Cost:** zero for the Ship-B era (capital-live gated per D-168; the 25% discount returns at `.E.1` when the ExchangeRegistry carries a commission-asset→conversion-source column — BNB support = 1 row + a feed). **Codification (slate add, D-172):** EXTEND `defer-to-source-authority` spec + the `.E.1` foreach-exchange pattern with the convertibility rule at Ship-B close (canonical-sister discipline — no new spec; first canonical = Ship B's implementation). D-93 design-pass reading: Binance Commission FAQ + executionReport `n`/`N` semantics.

**D-190 (AMENDS D-105 — formula-SSoT, not just rounding-mode; surfaced by the `.E.0.10` adversarial audit, 2026-06-10).** D-105 correctly named the decimal-sensitive P&L cohort + the "live≠replay if not uniform" risk and applied ONE rounding MODE (half-even) uniformly — but that is NECESSARY, NOT SUFFICIENT: the gross FORMULA was left split. `DrainPostFill` (`ControllerEventLoop.hpp:1536`) computes `Money_Sub(Money_Mul(exit,qty), Money_Mul(entry,qty))` (2-mul) while `Portfolio_CloseSlot:395` + mode-0 `:880` + `EventLoop_OnEvent:1962` all use 1-mul `Money_Mul(Money_Sub(exit,entry),qty)`. Same mode ≠ same value: `round((a−b)q) ≠ round(aq)−round(bq)`. **PREEXISTING** (present in the FPN era `.E.0.6`; gap ~1e-19, invisible) → the P2b decimal flip (838bf09) ACTIVATED it to 1 ULP (1e-8) on ~25% of fills, accumulating → `core_realized` ≠ `oms.realized_pnl`. D-105's "correctness-WIN, not a present bug (0 CRITICAL)" grade was true under FPN truncation, FALSE under decimal — a "not-a-bug" judgment that didn't survive the representation change. ALSO: D-105's cohort list (`:1959-1967` + `:862-890`) MISSED the DrainPostFill mode-1 path (`:1528-1540`) — the outlier. **FIX (the missing half of D-105):** ONE canonical `Money_FillGross(entry,exit,qty)` (1-mul = the authoritative books) called by all gross sites → reconcile BY CONSTRUCTION (surgical: 1 outlier site, or the helper + 3 inline sites). + golden regen (D-105 already flagged) + adversarial input-sweep proof + sweep for sibling parallel-derivations + the fee-rederivation sister (`:877` re-derives from cfg vs DrainPostFill consumes pre_resolved). Codified: memories `feedback_single_source_the_computation_not_just_the_mode` + `feedback_passing_test_is_not_verification` (the green-test-as-done audit-failure that masked it). PARITY entry + LANDMINE (decimal epoch activates latent parallel-derivation divergence) owed.

**D-202 REFINEMENT (same session — operator clarified the tool's PURPOSE: "the data flow is the entire point… how changing a base struct impacts the consumers down the line… realignment and work downstream since the structs that consume it will be smaller or larger").** The HEADLINE purpose of Tool A is **struct-change → downstream-impact analysis**, NOT primarily orphan-detection. Given a base-struct/type change (resize, re-pack, type-swap), trace the **transitive composition cascade** (who contains it → who contains them, recursively) + the **per-consumption-site** offset / cache-line / realignment delta. Orphan-detection (the 4 sub-shapes A9/A11/A12/A16/A24) is a **secondary query** over the same field-dependency graph, not the headline. This **disentangles two data-flows my earlier v1/v2 framing wrongly conflated:** (1) **composition/layout data-flow = v1 CORE** — tractable (libclang record-layouts + transitive containment + `pahole` cache-line spans; the deep/transitive/layout-precise AST successor to `gen_code_map --structs T`, which today is grep + one-level only); (2) **value-liveness data-flow = v2** — escape/reachability for the discarded-copy/dead-path A9 shape (the only genuinely-hard part). Concrete cascade already in-code: the Ship-A 16B flip shrank `OrderPreResolved<64>` 48B→32B (`Order.hpp:148` static_assert "Ship-A 16B: 48→32") → cascades to `Order`/`Position`/ExecutionCore params + their consumption sites = exactly TD-159's sharpened scope. **Two sharpenings:** (a) track OFFSET/SPAN deltas, not just SIZE — a pure H6 field-reorder shifts cache-line spans at constant size; (b) the tool's real signal is **FILTERING the cascade to the sites that need actual WORK** — most consumption sites are compiler-handled (offsets recomputed, no source change); the WORK sites are the **byte-layout-sensitive** (memcmp/SHA/wire/HMAC — H9/H12) + **cache-sensitive** (hot-path cache-line straddle / `alignas(64)` false-sharing — H6) ones. **Tool B = the SISTER, precisely:** Tool A = the STATIC structural impact (what resizes, WHERE realignment is needed — *scopes* the rework + the sites); Tool B = the codegen/cycle verification (did the rework hit minimum cycles / no new splits — *verifies* the result). Same underlying data (the field + hot-set), two halves: plan-the-rework / verify-the-rework. So Tool A unifies, at AST depth, `gen_code_map --structs` (the composition/layout half) + the mechanized `/dependency-chain-trace` (the consumption-flow half).

**D-202 ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW OUTCOME (2026-06-12 — 3-agent independent FIND/REFUTE panel on the design spec; operator-directed "adversarial review of the spec using the associated skills").** Verdict **RED — do not build as-is.** The SEQUENCING (tools-first; Tool-X before `.E.1`; fix-ship as dogfood corpus) HOLDS — none of the findings touch it. The TOOL ARCHITECTURE was sent back: (1) `gen_code_map` ALREADY ships transitive composition (`--composition`, `:108-142`) + `--byte-context` + `--macros` → the "v1 novelty" is largely built; this spec's "grep + one level" characterization was a canonical-sister-inspection MISS (the very `feedback_audit_canonical_sister_before_new_infra` failure, ironic given D-202 invoked it); (2) the CI "layout-sensitivity golden" is inferior to + duplicates the existing compile-time `offsetof`/`sizeof` static_assert idiom (43 present; `check_struct_alignment.py` already recommends extending them); (3) libclang exposes ZERO field cursors for the X-macro structs (`PerCoreCfg`/`ControllerConfig<F>`) that are the primary targets → the AST core is blocked on its headline target (needs the registry-text-parse existing tools already do); (4) `function→thread` is ill-posed (same OMS fns run drainer-live + single-threaded-backtest) → v1's cache filter needs call-context reachability → the v1/v2 split is dishonest; (5) Tool B's `llvm-mca` is absent + ill-suited to inlined header-only code (`pahole -R` on the existing `-g` builds + `/latency-track` carry the signal). **The review VALIDATED its own worth** — it caught precisely the over-build the adversarial-default exists to catch. **Re-cut direction (pending operator):** a thin `cascade <T>` composer over `gen_code_map --composition`/`--byte-context`/`--macros` + `pahole -R`; a static_assert-COVERAGE meta-guard (not a golden) for "catch what I forget"; `/latency-track` for the codegen half (drop `llvm-mca`); the orphan-detection (A9/A11/A12/A24) as the one genuinely-new + genuinely-hard piece (liveness + call-graph + registry-parse), honestly scoped. NOT proceeding to build (consult-before-coding; the "proceed-if-green" condition was not met).

**D-202 GAP-FILL LANDED (2026-06-12) — the proportionate realization (NOT the libclang engine the review killed).** Operator: "a + b + gap fills... more work now means less later." (a) `check_struct_alignment.py` extended with **check (c) byte-serialization size-pin coverage** (H9/H12 — an in-tree type serialized via fwrite/fread/memcmp/SHA/HMAC MUST carry `static_assert(sizeof(T)==N)`; ENFORCING via pre-commit Check K) + the D-137 self-test (teeth on BOTH (a) + (c)) + an explicit `sizeof(Money)==16` pin (avoids fragile alias resolution). (b) the cascade-check **trigger** in `CoreFrameworks/CLAUDE.md` (core-struct-layout change → run `gen_code_map --composition`/`--byte-context` + `clang -fdump-record-layouts`; pahole chokes on `<F>` templates). **VERIFIED:** self-test PASS (RED-on-violation + GREEN-on-fix, both classes); real-tree GREEN (4 in-tree serialized types pinned: Money/FPN_Binary/Position/BanditState); build 3372/0; doc sweep CLEAN. The by-context verification CAUGHT + FIXED a false-negative in the new guard (`struct alignas(64) Position` made `TYPE_DEF` capture "alignas" not "Position" → an alignas wire-struct would be silently skipped) — the discipline working. **What the review established:** the cascade-impact analysis is ~80% already shipped (`gen_code_map --composition`/`--byte-context`/`--macros`); the genuinely-missing pieces were ENFORCEMENT (the coverage guard) + DISCOVERABILITY (the trigger) — both landed cheaply. **STILL DEFERRED (own ship, genuinely-hard):** the orphan-detector (liveness + call-graph; its A9/A11/A12/A24 bugs are already in the fix-ship) + Tool B codegen-verifier (TD-177, rides TD-159 post-`.E.1`). Spec marked stage-2 + RED-review-banner + re-cut direction. Now proceeding to the `.E.0.10` fix-ship (A9 first, with sign-off per consult-before-coding).

**D-203 (pessimistic-by-DEFAULT: fold a conservative non-zero slippage default into A9 + ratify the adversarial-sim principle as CORE, 2026-06-12).** Operator, verbatim: *"id rather be pessimistic by default... people would respect a backtest that survives after trying to make it fail every way possible than get a single highest return on average, because if you can force it to work after making it fail in more ways than the market can you can trust it."* **DECISION:** (1) **A9 verified-done** (2-agent adversarial refute CLEAN — sign / live-gating / no-double-slip / backtest-coverage / OnEvent-deletion-safety all verified; char-test 3-lens, values independently re-derived 60060/60939; suite 3371/0). (2) **Fold-in:** resolve the `slippage_pct` default conflict — `ControllerConfig.hpp:1661` (`Money_Zero()`, "disabled/backward-compat") vs the registry `0.05` — to a CONSERVATIVE NON-ZERO default, so paper/backtest are pessimistic BY DEFAULT (A9 wired the consumption; this makes the sim honestly-costed out of the box, not opt-in). (3) **`adversarial-pessimistic-simulation-discipline.md` RATIFIED as core** — the trustworthiness thesis is the error-cost asymmetry (a false-positive costs capital; a false-negative costs only opportunity) + the operator framing above (survives-every-failure-mode > max-average-return-on-an-optimistic-sim). Queued for promotion to a DESIGN_PHILOSOPHY priority gradient. **Operator migration impact (ratified):** setting a non-zero default slippage IS a deliberate behavior change to EVERY paper/backtest result (P&L drops by the modeled execution cost) — existing backtests re-run pessimistic; any golden/regression baseline that assumed zero-slip regenerates once (epoch-style). This is the intent, not a regression.

**D-204 (A25 trail-trigger = the SET-SITE structural fix, NOT my proposed read-site relocate; + the combined investigate→adversarial meta-lesson, 2026-06-12).** A25: trailing-SL activation gates on the absolute expected-entry-priced `original_tp` while the exit fires from the per-fill `live_tp` — diverges under slippage (now LIVE post-A9). **REJECTED my own recommendation (A) relocate `ResolvePerFillTpPct` → shared `StrategyInterface.hpp`** — the adversarial agent showed it's a FORBIDDEN WIDE CASCADE (StrategyInterface is a lean enum header consumed by 24 TUs incl. GUI/TUI/bandit that don't include FixedPoint; relocating forces 1900-line `__int128` money math into all 24 — the `DESIGN_PHILOSOPHY.md:726` boundary-stable-over-wide-cascade anti-shape + `feedback_reduce_touch_sites`). Also rejected (B) pass-tp_pct-through-4-signatures (unnecessary plumbing). **DECIDED: the SET-SITE fix** — decision-time-bind the per-fill `tp_pct` (resolved via A1's `ResolvePerFillTpPct` at `BuildParameters`, already in `pending_params.tp_pct` + reachable there) → carry onto the Order → at `handle_buy_fill` set `original_tp = fill_price × (1 + tp_pct)`. All 4 read-sites + the exit-bandit correct with ZERO edits. Structural-fix-at-root (one producer, all consumers correct) mirroring A9 (bind-at-submit/consume-at-fill) + A1 (resolver SSoT); the cycle never arises (resolve where reachable, carry the value). **Bandit conflict RESOLVED by code-read** (the 2 agents disagreed — investigative said the set-site change BREAKS the bandit, adversarial said FIXES): the bandit (`ControllerEventLoop.hpp:1749`) computes `tp_pct=(original_tp−entry)/entry` wanting the intended pct; the current expected-entry-priced `original_tp` yields a slip-DISTORTED pct, the per-fill `original_tp` yields the CLEAN pct aligned with where the exit fires (and stays locked-at-entry/non-ratcheted, the property the bandit comment cares about) → FIXES it. **Hot-swap: NO impact** (dlopen ABI = `extern "C"` 4 symbols, NOT StrategyInterface; the fix is engine-owned `Position` state — `.E.X` "preserved, NOT in .so"); **do-now in `.E.0.10`** (touches nothing in the strategy-interface contract; `.E.X` inherits a lean StrategyInterface precisely because we did NOT relocate into it). **META-LESSON → TECH_DEBT-178:** I (proposer) recommended the cascade-(A); an INVESTIGATIVE agent independently endorsed a relocate path; ONLY the independent ADVERSARIAL pass caught the forbidden cascade — and I'd SKIPPED it until operator pushback ("did you run adversarial agents?"). The investigate→adversarial sequence must be ONE combined method so the adversarial half isn't momentum-skipped (the exact failure `feedback_adversarial_framing_default_for_checks` names) — adversarial-verify DECISIONS/proposals, not only findings/tests.

**D-205 (A25 CARRY mechanism dogfood + the agents' shared blind spot + my resolve-by-fiat meta-failure; D-204 premise RE-CONFIRMED, NOT superseded, 2026-06-12).** Dogfooded the new `/decision-check` (TD-178) on A25's IMPLEMENTATION carry-path (how `tp_pct` reaches the fill site), then — on operator's "is it wired properly / more than 2 places?" — ran a wiring agent + an exit-behavior agent. **Settled facts (code-verified):** (1) `Order`/`OrderPreResolved`/`SubmitCommand` are in-flight SPSC-only, NOT persisted — the persisted struct is the separate, versioned `OrderEvent` → a sizeof change is benign (no H9/H21 wire break), only the self-imposed `static_assert`s + a modest drainer cache line. (2) The cfg is NOT in scope at `handle_buy_fill` (`OrderManager.hpp:1171` has only `oms,o,fill,qty,booked_fee`) — it IS at submit (`OrderManager_Submit:917→982→Order_BindPreResolved:983`), so **bind-at-submit is forced** (the A9 decision-time-binding sister). **CARRY DECISION:** bind `pre_resolved.tp_pct = ResolvePerFillTpPct(o->strategy_id, core_cfg)` in/after `Order_BindPreResolved` (reuses A1 SSoT; `strategy_id` set @969 before the bind @983), consume at `handle_buy_fill`/`Portfolio_OpenSlot`. `OrderPreResolved` 32→48, `Order` 256→272 — **ACCEPTABLE** (in-flight-only; conscious assert bump @Order.hpp:148/403; drainer ≤10μs path, not the ≤500ns per-tick hot path; no 16B-aligned spare slot exists — `_pad_hot1` is 9B, H4 forbids a sub-Money repr).

**THE CORRECTION (I was wrong; D-204 STANDS).** Mid-synthesis I reframed A25 to a NARROW "bandit-counterfactual-only fix, no struct growth" on the claim that `original_tp` feeds only the exit-bandit + display on the sharded path. **That claim is FALSE.** The sharded per-strategy trail `Strategy_ExitAdjustPerCore` (`StrategyLifecycle.hpp:336`), dispatched on the sharded slow path at **`ControllerEventLoop.hpp:2716`** (coexists with the generic `EventLoop_TrailingSLRatchetOneCore` per the :2712 comment), fans out to `*_ExitAdjustSharded` — all 4 of which READ `original_tp` as the trail-arming gate (`Momentum.hpp:373`, `MeanReversion.hpp:565`, `MLStrategy.hpp:312`, `EmaCross.hpp:216`: `if (!Money_Gt(current_price, orig_tp)) continue;`). So A25's ORIGINAL premise holds: post-A9, expected-entry-priced `original_tp` ≠ fill → the 4 sharded trails arm at the wrong price + the bandit counterfactual is distorted. **A25 = D-204's set-site fix, fixing BOTH the trail readers AND the bandit. Not superseded.**

**META-FAILURE (codify — sister to AR-8).** Two agents DISAGREED on a code fact (wiring agent: trail reads `original_tp` + matters; behavior agent: `original_tp` is bandit+display-only). I resolved the disagreement **by FIAT — picking the convenient narrow-fix narrative** — the EXACT Stage-3 violation `/decision-check` encodes ("the agents can BOTH be wrong; resolve by code-read, never rubber-stamp"). The contradiction with THIS finding register (line 204: "all 4 **sharded** strategies") is what forced the deciding code-read, which vindicated the wiring agent + the register. **Both agents ALSO shared a blind spot** (keyed on the generic `TrailingSLRatchetOneCore`, missed the per-strategy `*_ExitAdjustSharded` dispatch). **Lesson:** a cross-check disagreement on a CODE FACT has a mechanical resolution (read the disputed code) — never "pick the cleaner story." NEW meta-anti-pattern candidate: **resolve-agent-disagreement-by-fiat** (sister to AR-8 self-attested-verification); strengthens `feedback_adversarial_framing_default_for_checks` + `/decision-check` Stage 3 (make the code-read MANDATORY when agents disagree on a fact, not advisory). Humbling: committed the skill's own named failure in the session I built the skill.

Gap = 3 load-bearing pieces ABSENT (ride-past-TP / per-position peak / give-back-reversal); ~30% reusable (the `ratchet_sl` rising-floor machinery `Money_Max(sl,ratchet_sl)` + the slow-path cadence walk + `original_tp` as the "old TP" anchor). **Cheapest struct plan (cascade agent, `-fdump-record-layouts`-verified):** keep peak-tracking SLOW-path + route give-back through the EXISTING `ratchet_tp`/`ratchet_sl` channel → ZERO hot-gate change, ZERO GateParameters/ExecutionCore growth; only ONE forced `Position` 128→192B (persisted PEAK + a riding-flag bit), **coordinate with D-55 (Position AoS→SoA at `.E.1`)**. **#1 RISK = the warm-restart re-arm** (`ShardedSnapshotPersist.hpp:591-687`) silently dropping peak/riding-state on restore = the A1 bug class; + 2–3 divergent `Position` persist geometries (`Portfolio_Save` prefix vs `ShardedSnapshotPersist` full vs legacy `PortfolioController`); + `is_trailing` is a DERIVED GUI signal at 2 sites that a riding-flag breaks. **Audit-tier: HIGH-RISK** (touches the H7/H8 branchless hot gate; the learned-ratchet must stay deterministic/seeded + warm-restart-correct + the LEARNING slow-path-only, the gate branchless-consume-only). **Framework-fit:** the learned ratchet EXTENDS the existing bandit substrate (`exit_bandits` + `EnsembleModelZoo` + Thompson; `Order::flags_packed` already carries `bandit_chosen_arm`) — new arms = ratchet parameterizations (1-row-in-registry), NOT new infra (sister-check per `feedback_audit_canonical_sister_before_new_infra`). A25 (fill-priced `original_tp`) is a PREREQUISITE (the redesign ratchets SL *to* `original_tp` → it must be fill-priced first).

The contradiction with the finding register forced the deciding code-read, which vindicated the register. **DECISION:** (1) NEW meta-anti-pattern **AR-11** (cross-check-disagreement-resolved-by-fiat + the shared-blind-spot corollary — independent agents CAN converge on the same wrong answer; sister to AR-8). (2) **Structural Stage-3 hardening** of `/decision-check`: when agents disagree on a CODE FACT (not a judgment), the orchestrator MUST resolve it by reading the disputed code ITSELF before surfacing — the code-read is MANDATORY, not "hand it to the operator." **D-209 (per-node position-ownership model + boot-reconcile recovery — venue-net foundation @`.E.1`, sub-pool deferred; A18 reverted; 2026-06-12).** The A18 guard (defending A25's `original_tp` against a BUY overwriting an active slot) broke `tsa-live-2` — it flips the boot-reconcile collapse from last-write-wins → first-write-wins (arguably worse for recovery), colliding with an OPEN issue (TECH_DEBT-072). Operator dialogue ("is this a storage/access problem? can a pool+bitmap dissolve oldest-vs-newest?") surfaced the real root cause + a better model. **DECISION:** (1) **A18 REVERTED** — re-homed to the reconcile rework (its only defensible form = "scope the guard to the LIVE async path, not reconcile replay," subsumed below). (2) **Root cause (code-grounded):** NOT memory/access-time — the storage is already pool+bitmap + uniform-access. It's *attribution + reassembly semantics*: at boot the in-flight order table is empty → fills can't be attributed to a node → all default to `core 0` (`Reconcile.hpp:245`) → `Portfolio_OpenSlot` OVERWRITES (vs net-aggregate) → N fills collapse to 1. (3) **`.E.1` foundation (build):** venue-net reconcile (reconstruct each node's net position from venue truth, not fill-replay) + explicit `owner_node_id` on `Position` + SoA — coordinate with **D-55** (Position AoS→SoA) + **D-206** (peak field) so Position storage opens ONCE. Fixes the collapse WITHOUT a storage-model rework. (4) **Future expansion (defer, NOT speculative):** per-node sub-pools (K slots/node + owner-tag + per-node mask; slot==core is K=1) — dissolves the collapse + the partial-leg special-case + enables scaling-in/out; build WHEN scaling earns it (the `owner_node_id` foundation makes it a 1-row-style add). **Captured:** LIVING DESIGN_SPEC `data-disciplines/per-node-position-ownership-model.md` (§ Future expansion = the gated growth) + TECH_DEBT-188 + the `future-expansion`/`reconcile-recovery`/`scale-invariance` tag-vocab additions. Proportionality (`feedback_overengineering_boundary_when_future_easier` cutting BOTH ways): the bug → venue-net (cheap); the general mechanism → deferred to a real need.

**THE A24 DECISION → option (c), the structural close (NOT the bare register-lean (a)).** Verdict 🟡 YELLOW on bare (a); (b) REFUTED; the complete fix is (c). The bug (triply-confirmed: my scout + both gate agents + a direct read of `ControllerConfig_ResolveForCore`): `resolved_cfg = ControllerConfig_ResolveForCore(*config, slot)` (`ControllerEventLoop.hpp:2303`) is a STACK-LOCAL fresh-per-rebuild full cfg; the resolver (`ControllerConfig.hpp:1385-1417`) does `resolved = global` then overlays per-core overrides onto **FLAT fields only** — it NEVER writes `resolved.cores[slot]`. The D6/D10/spike mutations write the FLAT `resolved_cfg.volume_multiplier/entry_offset_pct` (`:2337/:2403/:2410`) + a local `spacing_cfg` copy (`:2951/:2960`); the primary strategy consumer reads `&resolved_cfg.cores[slot]` (`:2677`) → wrote A, read B → the mutations are silently inert. **Option (c) = ** (1) redirect the 3 mutation sites + their clamps to write `resolved_cfg.cores[slot].X` (= (a)'s mechanics; stack-local, zero concurrency/wire/persist cascade — the 3 fields are absent from every persisted struct, grep-clean on `ShardedSnapshot*`); (2) **repoint the GUI spacing diagnostic `:2974`** (it reads the FLAT `spacing_cfg.spacing_multiplier` while the gate `Strategy_SpacingOk@:2980` reads the slice → a LIVE display↔execution inversion, present-tense, operator-visible — MUST co-fix or it just flips direction); (3) **extend `check_per_core_registry_integrity.py` Check 10** to flag "a per-shard WRITE to a flat cfg field that has a `cores[]` slice" — the literal H22 CI-check candidate CLAUDE.md already names; makes the class un-reintroducible (guards compound); (4) **tombstone** the flat fields as "centralized-path-only," with full DELETION homed behind `PortfolioController` retirement (9 legacy `ctrl->config.X` readers + the WIP2f `core_overrides` cascade — TECH_DEBT-191); (5) home the third-storage finding (below) separately so A24 does NOT false-close. **(b) REFUTED** — syncing flat→slice keeps two storages live = `cfg-scope-discipline.md` Anti-pattern 1 (dual-storage-with-manual-sync the next mutation-add forgets); strictly dominated by (a). **AR-11 factual disagreement resolved by orchestrator code-read (the gate's Stage-3 hardening working).** The investigative agent said MR/Momentum read the SLICE (`core_cfg->volume_multiplier`); the adversarial agent said they read a THIRD storage (`state->live_vol_mult`). Closing A24 as "the D6/D10 safety loop is live for all cores" would be a FALSE close → the third-storage gap is its own homed finding (TECH_DEBT-189).

**Gate findings BEYOND the register (the fan-out earned its tokens):**
- **Display↔execution inversion (HIGH, folded into A24)** — `:2974` GUI diag reads flat (sees the spike relaxation); `:2980` gate reads slice (doesn't) → the operator's diagnostic advertises a spacing floor the engine isn't enforcing. Fixed as part of A24 step (2).

The direction (per-node purity, framework extensibility) argues `live_*` is the right pattern and the cfg-mutation block is vestigial → CONVERGENCE is a `.E.1`/`.E.M` design decision (touches the strategy-state structs `.E.1`'s SoA pass opens anyway).

It's one-position-per-NODE (`per-node-position-ownership-model.md`: slot==core K=1, +1 partial leg); a CLUSTER = a group of nodes. node-0(MR/BTC) + node-1(SimpleDip/BTC) can BOTH be long BTC@65000 BY DESIGN (independent shards, separate capital + exits, H22 purity) — they don't "overlap" as conflicting orders. BUT the cluster's AGGREGATE exposure (N nodes long BTC = N× concentration) is governed by NOTHING — per-node purity makes each node correct, nobody owns the cluster aggregate. A real `.E.1`+ risk-aggregation layer (the `.E.1` aggregator already publishing the account snapshot is the natural home).

**THE D6/D10/spike REDESIGN DIRECTIONS (operator-driven; FLESHED OUT in `subplans/2026-06-12-v5.15.5.F.4d.1.E.M-adaptive-entry-redesigns.md`).** A24 fixes the PLUMBING (the mutation reaches the consumer); these decide the POLICY (what the mechanism should BE). The plumbing fix is correct regardless of the redesigns and UNBLOCKS them (can't redesign a mechanism whose output is discarded).
- **D6 (session volume multiplier) → baseline-free rolling volume normalization (KEEP, redesign).** Operator insight: the session approach hardcodes "Asian=quiet/US=busy" — a fixed baseline that assumes the time-zone profiles always hold. Replace with a self-normalizing design that needs NO hardcoded baseline: scale the volume-gate multiplier by current volume vs its OWN recent rolling distribution (relative-volume RATIO, or a z-score over a long EWMA window). The "normal" becomes the adaptive rolling baseline — never maintained, never wrong. Fits the existing `RollingStats`/`FlowFeatures`/`RegimeSignals` machinery (the ML extensibility point) → D6 stops being a cfg-mutation heuristic and becomes a proper normalized FEATURE the strategy consumes. Worth a design session; homes `.E.M` (or a standalone feature improvement — it's NOT bandit-gated).
- **D10 (losing-streak brake) → keep + paper-test measure now; learned version later.** The safety governor (negative P&L slope → tighten entries; positive → loosen). A24 makes it LIVE for the slice-readers; then the paper test measures whether the re-activation helps (it's an ALPHA/behavior change, not a pure correctness fix — dead since `49649b8` for SimpleDip/EmaCross/ML, so the engine's current tuning is WITHOUT it; re-activation has a faint Knight-Capital flavor → measure, don't trust). The learned version (bandit-driven brake) = `.E.M`, A11/A12-gated.
- **spike (spike-relaxation spacing) → bandit-learned per-strategy (heavier design pass).** Operator insight: a hardcoded uniform spacing-relaxation is the wrong shape when strategies have different hold-out patterns; "should this node relax spacing during a spike?" should be a LEARNED per-strategy decision, not a constant. Fits the existing bandit substrate (`exit_bandits` + Thompson + `EnsembleModelZoo`); D-206 already established "a learned adaptation = new bandit ARMS, not new infra." So learned-spacing = new arms in the existing substrate. **HARD DEPENDENCY: A11/A12** — the bandit reward-attribution producers are orphaned (`MBS_OrderSetBanditContext` defined-but-uncalled; `bandit_reward_bps` never written → reward dataset all-zeros) → the bandit can't learn ANYTHING until A11/A12 land. Homes `.E.M`, after A11/A12; thematically adjacent to D-206 (the learned-ratchet ship — may co-design).

**DEPENDENCY STRUCTURE + SEQUENCE (what makes this not balloon):** `A24 plumbing (slice-as-sole-storage + CI guard + H22 spec)` is foundational + branchless-neutral + INDEPENDENT of any redesign → lands first, unblocks all three. Then in parallel: `D6 feature redesign` (not bandit-gated) | `D10 keep+measure`; and `spike + learned-D10` gated on `A11/A12` → bandit substrate alive → `.E.M`. The cluster-aggregate-risk layer is separate (`.E.1`+). This is the "land the foundation, schedule the policy redesigns" split that keeps it vision-convergence not scope-balloon.

**DETERMINISM / BRANCHLESS / DOD CONSTRAINTS on the redesigns (operator-demanded; binding on `.E.M`):** any learned/adaptive mechanism MUST stay (a) deterministic — seeded, reproducible cross-run; (b) warm-restart-correct — the learned/normalized state persists AND restores (the A1 bug class — `live_*` is already persisted `Portfolio.hpp:575`, the model to follow); (c) the LEARNING is slow-path-only (≤100μs headroom), the HOT-path gate is branchless-consume-only (H7) — consume a seqlock-published param, never branch on learning state; (d) new per-node state lives in the cache-aligned per-node `slow_state`, clustered by access pattern (H6), cross-thread fields `alignas(64)` + seqlock. Tools RUN per design (verify-not-assert, operator-demanded): `/dod-audit` + `/hft-audit` per piece, `check_struct_alignment.py` for new-state layout/size-pin, `calls_graph_diff.sh` to prove the hot path stayed untouched, `/latency-track` for SP cost.

**D-212 (A16 partial-fill `/decision-check` → `.E.0.10` loud-STOP; full multi-fill reassembly FOLDS to `.E.1`; the OMS-fill-path scale-invariance reframe; 2026-06-13).** Operator-run `/decision-check` (2 armed halves — investigative + adversarial — routed `/accounting-audit` + `/trace-deps`, scout-first per M8) on A16's fix-approach, then an operator reframe elevating it to a foundational scale-invariance question. **Investigative half** recommended option (a) (gate-on-FILLED + accumulate `"l"` + weighted-avg + book-once-at-FILLED, OMS-internal). **Adversarial half REFUTED (a) as over-reach** (it IS `.E.1`'s venue-net-reconcile job — `per-node-position-ownership-model.md` says "reconstruct from venue truth, NOT replay fill-by-fill"; depends on the un-parsed cumulative `"z"` = A2/D-123; needs net-new weighted-avg machinery on the capital path), refuted (b) (`Portfolio_AddQuantity` doesn't re-weight `entry_price` → corrupts P&L), and showed bare (c) is WRONG (re-enters `handle_buy_fill` → `Portfolio_OpenSlot` overwrite + double `entry_fee` + re-arm A25 `original_tp`). **AR-11 orchestrator code-read RESOLVED the factual crux → adversarial CORRECT:** `o->filled_qty` is per-invocation (only 4 refs — `Order.hpp:179` comment "running total across partials" is ASPIRATIONAL/never-implemented [Class-38]; `OrderManager.hpp:1419` overwrite; `:1440` read-by-value to `HandleFill`), so the capital-loss is the **unconditional slot-free at `:1465` (slot-lifecycle bug), NOT qty-accumulation**; `handle_buy_fill` has NO active-slot guard (`handle_sell_fill:1210` does) + `Portfolio_OpenSlot` overwrites → any 2nd-event book corrupts.

**VERDICT (recommended; pending operator confirm):**
- **A16 `.E.0.10` = the loud-STOP:** gate BOTH `HandleFill` (`:1438`) AND the slot-free (`:1465`) on `Order_GetState(o)==ORDER_FILLED`; on `ORDER_PARTIAL` keep the slot open, **book nothing**, emit a loud degrade-arm warn ("multi-fill accumulation rides D-123; live sizing untrustworthy pre-`.E.1`" — the `A3 SHORTFALL`/D-173 degrade-warn precedent). A LOUD STOP (no silent slot-drop, no double-book, operator-visible), NOT a correct size. + fix the false `Order.hpp:179` comment. NOT option (a) — that's `.E.1`'s job.
- **A17 = co-land (CRIT deadlock) + extend to the REST adapter:** paper synthetic fill (`~:1013`) + the REST adapter (`BinanceAdapter.hpp:204-218`) BOTH never set `order_complete` → both work today only via the unconditional free → gating the free on FILLED without A17 deadlocks the slot. Set `order_complete=1` on terminal in BOTH (paper atomic + REST `executedQty==requested`/status==FILLED). Also dedup partial accumulation on `"t"` trade-id when the `.E.1` reassembly lands (WS-reconnect double-book guard).
- **A36 = OPERATOR CALL** (separate from A16 — sharded "partial EXIT" = 2 distinct orders/slots ≠ A16's "partial FILL" = 1 order/N increments; over-lump): **tombstone** (H21 — over-reach + touches the D-206 exit-redesign cascade; my lean, re-home to D-206) vs **wire** (default-ON risk feature, inert on sharded, has a proven wired sibling `BREAKEVEN_ON_PROFIT` @ `EngineCommon.hpp:732`, slow-path so no H7/H8 cost).

**THE SCALE-INVARIANCE REFRAME (operator, 2026-06-13 — "this is more foundational than I thought; does it scale N nodes × N clusters × N venues? did we forget?"):** the OMS fill path is a CORE foundational surface that must scale per-node × per-cluster × per-venue. **FINDING (grounded by reading the `.E.1` foundation plan + the exchange-adapter specs): `.E.1` ALREADY designs the scale-invariant fill-path** — per-node owns its OWN fill consumption (item 53; the central drainer is ABSORBED → Class 26 dead structurally), per-cluster shared producer/adapter/WS (item 58), the O(1) event-sourced global aggregator (item 62), the `FOREACH_EXCHANGE` adapter framework with a per-venue fill parser + `fill_callback` normalization boundary (the adapter-implementation-contract), and venue-net reconcile (`per-node-position-ownership-model.md`). **AND `.E.1` item 54 EXPLICITLY scopes the partial/submit failure-path to `.E.1`** (it folded A3 the same way: "`.E.0.10` pins only the STABLE happy-path; characterize the failure path HERE per two-foundations"). **So: we did NOT forget the scaling — it lives in `.E.1`; the `.E.0.10` loud-STOP is consistent with that design; A16's full multi-fill reassembly FOLDS INTO the `.E.1` submit-path rework (alongside A3/A2/A5/D-123 — the partial-fill cluster). NO new/scrapped design needed — `.E.1` has the shape.** The foundational-structure answer (operator priority per [[feedback-proactive-novel-alternative-consideration]] foundational-level): the better structure = `.E.1`'s per-cluster-pure, protocol-agnostic-fill-event, venue-adapter-normalized fill path; building fill-by-fill accumulation at `.E.0.10` (option a) would be the exact anti-pattern the per-node-ownership discipline warns against, torn out at `.E.1`.

**Structural principles (for the `.E.1` fill-path design pass + new anti-pattern candidate):** (1) **protocol-agnostic fill event at the OMS boundary** — the venue adapter normalizes WS-per-increment / REST-cumulative / FIX → ONE common fill event; the OMS runs ONE state machine that never knows the protocol. **Anti-pattern to track: protocol-specific handling leaking into the protocol-agnostic OMS core** (= today's bug — `ProcessFillCommand` implicitly assuming a shape). (2) **per-cluster-pure OMS** — H22 one level up (each cluster owns its fill path; scales to N clusters). (3) **idempotency/dedup (`"t"`) load-bearing at N venues**; venue-net reconcile = the scale-invariant recovery safety net. (4) **determinism/parity** — normalized fill event + deterministic state machine = backtest=paper=live. (5) **latency-neutrality (operator concern, 2026-06-13 — "does normalization add latency? this feels bad"):** VERIFIED a NON-issue + folded as a `.E.1` design CONSTRAINT, not a blocker — the fill path is POST-TRADE/async (WS/REST callback, ms-bounded by venue RTT), NOT the ≤500ns hot path (`BG/SG_Evaluate`) nor the ≤10μs drainer-submit; the hot path reads the seqlock-PUBLISHED position, unaffected. Normalization is at-PARSE (the adapter must parse the venue message regardless → normalizing into a common POD is the same work). Dispatch is COMPILE-TIME `tt::<verb><ExchangeT>` (H13 monomorphization, NO vtable — "protocol-agnostic" ≠ runtime-polymorphic). So: POD fill event (no alloc, H1) + compile-time dispatch (no vtable, H2) + slow-path state machine (off hot path) = ZERO hot-path cost. The `.E.1` fill-path design pass MUST verify these three hold (POD · compile-time dispatch · slow-path), not assume them.

**HOMED, not open tech debt:** A16-full-reassembly → the `.E.1` submit-path rework (AMEND the `.E.1` plan, fold A16 alongside A3 in item 54 — pending); the loud-STOP → `.E.0.10`. The deep `.E.1` fill-path design pass (cascade checks + scale-invariance lens + adversarial/investigative agents) fires when `.E.1` is the active ship.

- **A21 (iterate — bigger) — position-aware `Money` formula + GATE-THE-WRITE.** Code-verified keystone: `oms->balance` is FLAT-ACCOUNT value (start + Σ realized), NOT free cash — a BUY never debits it (`handle_buy_fill` `OrderManager.hpp:1177-1205` = zero balance write; sole mutations = `handle_sell_fill` + the reconcile clobber). So the reconciler comparing it against venue FREE-USDT diverges the instant a position opens. The handoff's "don't clobber while `active_bitmap!=0`" BLINDS detection exactly when capital is at risk (a genuine venue divergence during an open position → silently undetected; worse than the false-trip). Foundational = **asset-agnostic cash-leg** `expected_free_cash = balance − Σ_open(entry_price·qty + entry_fee) − Σ_inflight((requested−filled)·price + est_fee)`, in `Money` (**H4** — the reconciler ran the whole drift band in `double`), DETECTION always-on, GATE THE WRITE (alert+counter while `active_bitmap!=0`; authoritative correction → `.E.1` venue-net-reconcile). - **A20 (iterate — wrong target) — seed-don't-replay, not persist.** Code-verified: the OMS snapshot is PAPER-MODE-ONLY (`Run.hpp:982` `if(!live_trading) load; else reconcile-from-exchange`). Real fix = **seed-don't-replay** at the boot reconcile (`Run.hpp:1014` hardcoded `since_trade_id=0`): cold boot seeds the watermark to `max(fetched trade_id)` WITHOUT replaying (exchange-seeded balance already reflects settled trades); warm recovery replays only the missed tail. <!-- D/C/F: D-217 -->
**D-217 (NEW-1 FULL fan-out result + the legacy-path Knight ruling + do-now confirmed → the split-brain codified RBP Class 47; 2026-06-13).** The 4-agent NEW-1 fan-out (2 investigative `/trace-deps` + 2 adversarial, AR-11 orchestrator code-reads) CONFIRMED the D-215 single-authority shape, found the bug is WORSE than framed, surfaced 6 safety items the fix must carry, and the operator's Knight-rule question flipped the legacy-path disposition. FINAL:

- **The bug is a SPLIT-BRAIN, not a redundant flag (→ Class 47).** `use_real_money` + `trading_mode` are two INDEPENDENT, never-cross-wired fields: `use_real_money` gates EXECUTION (the OMS `MASK_OMS_STATE_LIVE_TRADING` bit + secrets + adapter, `Run.hpp:577`); `trading_mode` gates ONLY the safety pre-flight (`LiveReadiness_Verify:230`) + the strict-flips (`NormalizeForMode:2009`). BOTH decoupling directions are live TODAY: `use_real_money=1 + trading_mode=paper` → **real capital, ENTIRE pre-flight bypassed**; `trading_mode=live + use_real_money=0` → pre-flight refuses boot, NO orders fire. The safety gate guards a door that isn't the real door.
- **Shape CONFIRMED (all 4 agents): single-authority predicate** `ControllerConfig_IsLiveCapital(cfg) := (cfg.trading_mode == TRADING_MODE_LIVE)`; repoint every authorizer; `use_real_money` → back-compat PARSE alias (`=1` promotes `trading_mode=LIVE` + warns) + tombstone the int (H21). **`trading_mode` is the right survivor** (registry-resident, STAMP_BOUND, SAFETY_CRITICAL, 3-state) vs `use_real_money` (an un-registry orphan bool that can't even express SHADOW).
- **Stamp-CLEAN (H9):** `use_real_money` is stamp-free (grep-confirmed absent from every registry) → retiring it changes ZERO HMAC bytes. `trading_mode` IS STAMP_BOUND but its emit is TRAIN-TIME-ONLY (all `Stamp_AssembleAndEmit` callers in `Backtest/`+`foxml_suite`) → the alias's parse-time mutation never perturbs a serve-time stamp. PIN this invariant (a future BOOT-time stamp re-emit would make the alias wire-affecting).
- **Operator's Knight-rule ruling → HARD-REFUSE the legacy capital path (NOT repoint).** The operator asked "does keeping legacy alive violate the Knight rule?" — it does: H21's core is *"remove dead code, don't leave it compiled-in (esp. dead capital-paths)"* (the Power Peg was a dead capital path left compiled-in). Legacy single_core LIVE WARN-continues (`main.cpp:386`, reachable). So the Knight-correct move is NOT repoint-and-maintain — it is TOMBSTONE: **hard-refuse legacy single_core LIVE at boot** (may run paper; cannot authorize real capital; sharded = the SOLE real-money authority). The ~10 legacy `use_real_money` reads repoint-to-predicate only so the field-deletion compiles (dead behind the refuse); full legacy-path removal homes to **TECH_DEBT-002**. (`feedback_backwards_compat_not_default_concern` + operator "we don't care about legacy".)
- **6 safety items the fix MUST carry (the fan-out's new hazards):** (1) **HotReload** — add `trading_mode` to the `PortfolioController.hpp:1964/1978` protected save/restore set (else a hot-reload bulk-copies a new `trading_mode` → flips capital authority MID-SESSION; the bug re-opened on the reload path). (2) **Alias ordering + conflict** — promote BEFORE `NormalizeForMode` (so the strict-flips arm); `use_real_money=1` + explicit `trading_mode=paper` → HARD boot REFUSE (needs a new `MASK_CFG_KEY_TRADING_MODE` explicit bit). (3) **GUI** — remove the `use_real_money` checkbox (`SettingsPanel.hpp:411/1084`); `trading_mode` is already registry-rendered, so leaving it lets an operator flip the derived bool independently (and `=0` won't demote → capital armed while the toggle reads off). (4) **Tooltip inversion (SAFETY_CRITICAL, live)** — `CfgFieldRegistry.hpp:400` says "1=PROD_SHADOW, 2=LIVE" but enum+parser are LIVE=1/SHADOW=2 → fix to "1=LIVE, 2=SHADOW" (an operator setting `=1` for "bracketed shadow" gets real-money LIVE). (5) **SHADOW** — cosmetic (one log-string reader; no execution branch) → tombstone RESERVED (keep value 2 per H21, it's stamp-wire-visible) + a boot WARN; do NOT implement. (6) update the 4 in-repo cfgs + README to `trading_mode=`.
- **Mode matrix (valid / FORBID):** PAPER×0 ✅ · PAPER×1 FORBID (capital, no pre-flight — the headline danger) · LIVE×0 FORBID (pre-flight, no orders) · LIVE×1 ✅ (the one true live cell) · SHADOW×0 ✅ · SHADOW×1 FORBID. Single-authority makes the 3 FORBID diagonals UNREPRESENTABLE (a guard could only DETECT them — the reason the boot-guard fallback is insufficient).
- **Do-now CONFIRMED (operator).** The predicate IS the `.E.1` per-cluster seam — at `.E.1` the body relocates `cfg.trading_mode`→`cluster.trading_mode`, ZERO authorizer-site edits (H22-positive; neither field has a `core_N_*` override → not A1-shaped). Deferring keeps the split-brain armed through the paper-test window (unacceptable, SAFETY_CRITICAL).
- **Captures:** RBP **Class 47** (split-brain control authority; sibling of Class 43 [one VALUE 2 ways] / Class 45 [reconstruct DIFFERENT source] — SSoT family at the control-flag/authority surface; Knight-adjacent to H21 = concurrent field-DUPLICATION vs identifier-reuse); DESIGN_SPEC **`single-authority-predicate-for-mode-gating.md`** (the fix pattern + the H22 scale-out seam; sisters `per-node-purity-scale-invariance.md` + `capital-allocation-policy-pattern.md`); refs H21/H22/glossary §15. The char-test = the anti-vacuous MODE MATRIX (a fixture where the two flags AGREE hides every bug — the A1-vacuity trap).

<!-- D/C/F: D-218 -->
**D-218 (NEW-1 conflict-handling RECONCILED to hard-refuse + the root-cause of the silent deviation + the decision↔code binding discipline; 2026-06-13, operator-caught via "are you sure we captured everything?").** A code-vs-decision DRIFT: D-217 safety-item #2 specified the `use_real_money=1` + explicit-`trading_mode≠LIVE` conflict → **HARD boot REFUSE** (ADV-2's call), but the shipped code did **explicit-wins-fail-safe** (boots paper + a warn). RESOLVED:

- **RECONCILED to hard-refuse (the decided behavior).** `ControllerConfig_Load` sets a new `live_capital_cfg_conflict` flag on the conflict; `main.cpp` REFUSES boot (`return 1`) BEFORE any engine dispatch — covers sharded + legacy; a contradictory capital config never runs. A flag (not `exit()`) so it stays unit-testable. The char-test rewritten **NON-VACUOUS**: asserts the flag IS set on conflict + NOT set on promote/agree (the prior `!IsLiveCapital` assertion was true for BOTH explicit-wins AND refuse — the vacuity that HID the drift). Build **3419/0**.
- **Root-cause (the REAL why — NOT compaction):** the drift happened in ONE continuous session (D-217 written, then coded ~20 turns later, no compaction between — I did not FORGET the decision). Mid-implementation I hit a mechanism friction (`ControllerConfig_Load` returns a cfg by value → a true hard-refuse needs a flag+boot-check; Load can't halt itself) and in execution-mode momentum took the easier path (explicit-wins, which also fails safe) WITHOUT looping back to D-217 or flagging the deviation. = an **effort-driven silent deviation from a merit-decided behavior** (`feedback_no_defer_for_effort` / `feedback_deferral_reasons_merit_not_effort_or_context` applied to a DEVIATION, not a defer → no trigger fired). It slipped because (a) the pinning test was VACUOUS + (b) `check_session_docs` verifies STRUCTURE (index/sentinels), NOT SEMANTICS (does the code match the decision) — a code-vs-decision drift sails through a green sweep.
- **Structural fix — decision↔code binding (M7 escalation).** A decision specifying a CODE BEHAVIOR is a CONTRACT bound to a NON-VACUOUS pinning test (drift → test failure); implementation deviations reconcile the decision-log + test in the SAME step. <!-- D/C/F: D-221 -->
**D-221 (A6 INGRESS reshape — detect-at-ingress NOT at the runtime fallback; graceful per-arm-drop + MAJORITY-SHALT; 5-pass verified; 2026-06-15, operator pair-programming session).** The fresh swarm to build the A6 ingress (per D-220) found the register's prescribed implementation site is STRUCTURALLY WRONG, then the operator reshaped the policy. FINAL:
- **The reshape (orchestrator code-read CONFIRMED):** the register said "branch corrupt-vs-missing at `StrategyParameters.hpp:801`." But a corrupt model collapses into `MODEL_LOAD_FAILED` at `EngineCommon.hpp:361` (`if (!loaded && !ensemble_loaded)`) and the `:800` `!CoreModelZoo_HasAny` guard early-returns to SimpleDip BEFORE `:801` — so a corrupt model silently degrades to SimpleDip, **the EXACT failure D-220 forbids.** Detection MUST move to the ingress (load-time), carried as an explicit signal. → **RBP Class 49** (distinguishing signal collapsed upstream of the branch that needs it).
- **Carrier = BOUNDED (resolved a 1-I→1-A factual disagreement by code-read, AR-11):** the A-class claimed a ~10-site cascade (out-param through `TryLoadRole`); the I-class found the per-arm barrier is copied at ONE site `ezoo_set_per_arm_barrier` (`CoreModelZoo.hpp:1123`, called once `:1836`) that BOTH boot (AutoDetect→LoadFromCfg) and hot-swap (ShadowLoad→LoadFromCfg, `HotSwap.hpp:134`) funnel through. Code-read confirmed the I-class: detect there, `corrupt_arms_mask` = **1 row in `FOREACH_PER_ARM_FLAG`**, NO `TryLoadRole` signature change. The A-class assumed the wrong detection site.
- **OPERATOR DECISIONS (locked this session):**
  1. **Graceful per-arm-drop → FULLY DISABLE the corrupt arm** (`disabled_horizon_mask`, not barrier-only — "rather retrain than have silent/bad models trading"). The surviving blend is clean by construction (a dropped arm's `arms_with_barriers_mask` bit is never set → masked out of `Σ weights·barrier`). A STICKY "arm corrupt — RETRAIN" alert fires on ANY drop (never silent — the load-bearing subtlety).
  2. **Whole-node SHALT = MAJORITY corrupt, NOT a fixed 66%.** Operator caught that a fixed 2/3 fails for odd N (5 models → never trips at 3-of-5). → `corrupt_ratio > 0.5` (strict majority; "more corrupt than healthy → halt"), configurable via `model_corrupt_shalt_ratio` default 0.5. All-corrupt (empty barrier-zoo) → flip `MODEL_LOAD_FAILED`→`MODEL_CORRUPT`. Arms cap = `ENSEMBLE_HORIZON_MAX=8` (the variable-N/cache tradeoff bound).
  3. **Block-new-only** (`GATE_FLAG_BUY_BLOCKED` gates entries; an open position rides its valid pre-corruption barriers). Covers BOTH sides: no new position → no corrupt exit-levels ever set; an already-open position exits on its valid barriers; the egress `FinalizeEmit` re-validates tp/sl EVERY rebuild as the cheap node last-line.
  4. **SANE-MAX = separate caps** (equity-agnostic — barrier is a FRACTION not a price, confirmed by real stamps reading `0.07`): **SL ≤ 1.0** (100% — economically hard, can't lose >100% spot); **TP ≤ a generous bound** ("uncapped up to a set amount given cache constraints" — a constant for now; the bit-pack-derived cap is the future-opt, TECH_DEBT task #12). `barrier_is_corrupt(tp,sl,max)` = ONE shared inline (SSoT) at ingress + trainer-emit.
  5. **Per-arm corrupt visibility** → explicit snapshot mirror (monitoring = control-plane-aligned).
- **Audit-refined constraints (DOD + HFT passes, both run as the DEDICATED skills after operator caught they hadn't been):** `model_corrupt_shalt_ratio` = `FPN_Binary<F>`+`KIND_DOUBLE` (H4, NOT bare double) in the "Drift Acknowledgments" cfg group; `MASK_CORE_STATE_MODEL_CORRUPT` = bit **5** (next-free, NOT 6); the bit MUST be set by the per-core SLOW-PATH thread only (single-writer `core_state_flags`), ordered AFTER the `__ATOMIC_ACQ_REL` `ensemble_handle` swap on that same thread (mirror `MODEL_LOAD_FAILED`); `static_assert(corrupt_arms_mask width ≤ ENSEMBLE_HORIZON_MAX)`; **`SHALT_MODEL_CORRUPT`** distinct from `SHALT_BAD_PCT` (quad-confirmed I/A/DOD/HFT — the hot path can't tell WHY it blocked, so attribution needs its own SHALT); trainer-fold at `StampHelper.hpp:258` ONLY (the field carries 3 unit conventions — stamp=fraction/RunConfig=percent/Panels=CSV → a wider guard false-rejects a legit `1.5`; the unit hazard homed to TECH_DEBT task #11); **blend-guard DROPPED** (redundant — a NaN blend → `money_from_double_payload`→`+sat` → the already-landed egress catches `>MAX_PCT`→BUY_BLOCKED); `tp_pct_b` egress clamp after `:1810` (the real leg-B leak — derived post-clamp, unbounded → `live_tp_b` up to 100,000×).
- **Verification (5 passes): 3-I→3-A (design) + 1-I→1-A (refined) + orchestrator code-read + `/dod-audit` (YELLOW→resolved) + `/hft-audit` (CLEAN).** Hot path 0ns delta (reads only seqlock `cached_params`, never `core_state_flags`; SHALT rides the existing branchless BUY_BLOCKED mask at `ExecutionCore.hpp:366-368`); H3/H7/H8/H22 ALL hold; replay-deterministic (pure function of stamp bodies + integer popcount). `calls_graph_diff` predicted CLEAN.
- **Captures (this session):** RBP **Class 49** (lossy-state-collapse); `plans/_future/2026-06-15-control-plane-data-plane-housekeeping-separation.md` (operator-raised: housekeeping/validation → controller tier, A6 already load-time-aligned, does NOT conflict with the `.E` decoupling endgoal); the 3-convention unit hazard (task #11); the **audit-skill meta-lesson** (memory `feedback_run_dedicated_audit_skills_not_just_armed_prompts` — armed prompts ≠ running the dedicated skill; operator-caught); the SANE-MAX/bit-pack heavier-look (task #12).

The **snapshot `VERSION`** (genuine binary/HMAC wire identifier — old snapshots on disk would be silently mis-read) KEEPS its H21 tombstone + version-bump. **D-229 — Foundation-instrument hardening BEFORE the E.1.0 build (the scoped A⁺ 3:3 + the size-miscalc exposed a structural guard gap; operator: harden the tools/agents/docs, then RE-SWEEP, then build).** The scoped A⁺ 3:3 + cascade + AR-11 verify RESOLVED the 3 open decisions AND exposed a foundational gap (unguarded hand-computed code-facts) that warrants hardening the investigation instruments before closing the foundation. **Decisions RESOLVED:** (1) the 4 reshapes RATIFIED (vacuity-proof FLAGS-not-labels gate [the degrade stamps `strategy_id=ML` `StrategyParameters.hpp:824` → a `strat=` check is vacuous; gate on `MODEL_LOAD_FAILED|MODEL_CORRUPT==0` + an inference-ran counter; → Class-51 first canonical]; BUILD the missing sharded per-core REBUILD/hot p99 emit FIRST [today `[OMS_BENCH]`=drainer-only]; floor capture → E.1.2; DROP the committed `latency_baseline.txt` [cycle-based=machine/turbo-specific + SSoT-dup with HOT_PATH_CHANGELOG] → run-relative); (2) deep-tail → DEFER to `.E.2` (ungateable in CI — the single-thread backtest substrate has no tail; `.E.2` already specs `fox_node_latency_hot_p9999_ns`; "expand the instrument"→`CoreLatencyStats` is the EXPENSIVE always-on one, 256→10k ring=640KB blows L1d; the cheap sister-spec builds a DIFFERENT bench-gated instrument = `.E.2`'s visibility deliverable, not a gate); (3) the slow-path optimization = a COMPUTE+CACHE job (11 Push + regression `ControllerEventLoop.hpp:2183-2204`, NOT `rolling_baseline` cache — 64KB w/ ~64-byte per-cycle hot set), DISJOINT cohort from E.1.2's Portfolio/Position SoA → its OWN `.E` leaf, NOT folded into E.1.2. **THE FOUNDATIONAL FINDING:** the `rolling_baseline` size comment ("~1.5MB" vs actual 65,984B/64.4KB) = `rolling_baseline × 16 cores at the OLD 24B FPN` (~1.44MB) — a PER-ENGINE total on a PER-CORE field; the 3 comments mix per-core/per-engine SCOPE + 24B/16B SIZE, never reconciled. Root: nothing guards a NON-serialized struct's size (`check_struct_alignment.py` pins only byte-serialized types). Generalizes to ALL hand-computed code-facts (size/cache-line/complexity/latency) — the "~100-300µs ROLLING" + "~5-30µs REBUILD" budgets are the SAME unverified-hand-comment class (the H8 bench MUST measure them; do NOT lock ROI/budgets on them). **SEQUENCE (operator-directed):** capture → harden instruments [size+cache-budget guard tool + static_assert retrofit · umbrella sweep (ALL hand-computed facts) · doc-fixes · subagent registry + custom agent types (I/A/cascade/V) + routing index (stop the general-purpose default) · the derived-facts-drift meta-pattern DESIGN_SPEC + Class-51 + cascade-bridge codifications] → RE-RUN the I→A sweep with the updated tools/agents (will expose more) → THEN build E.1.0. **Cache-residency (operator): codify a STATIC L1d/L2 budget guard (in the tool) + a DYNAMIC miss-rate dimension (in the H8-family bench) — "we're cache-resident" is itself unverified until measured.**

<!-- D/C/F: D-230 -->
**D-230 — CONTEXT (operator architectural direction; not yet a decision): cache-residency is already SMT-pinning-mitigated + the cluster owns the shared venue-interface AND shared market-state (DOS-driven, a live-readiness REQUIREMENT not just an optimization).** Captured to direct the cluster (E.1.3/E.1.5/E.1.6) + H8-bench + cache-budget-tool investigations. **Cache/L1d-pollution:** the hot-path-jitter-from-slow-path-L1d-pollution concern is REAL but ALREADY architected — `EngineSharded_SmartSlowPathPins` (`Run.hpp:204-264`) reads `/sys/.../thread_siblings_list` + pins slow-path threads AWAY from the SMT siblings of hot/producer/drainer (SMT siblings share L1d/L2) → hot+slow on different physical cores → no pollution. NO direct x86 L1d-pinning (HW-managed); levers = prefetch/non-temporal-stores/CAT(L3)/the SMT-pinning. CODIFY 3 layers: (1) the SMT-pinning discipline as a DESIGN_SPEC + a boot-time non-sibling assert (coded, not codified/tested); (2) the cache-budget tool extended to HOT-path structs (working set ≤ L1d); (3) the H8 bench reads cache-miss perf-counters (residency is UNVERIFIED until measured — the derived-facts-drift meta-pattern). Residual: pinning is best-effort (`Run.hpp:191` WARN) → isolcpus+HT-off is a DEPLOYMENT requirement, not a code guarantee; CAT is ops/root-gated. **Cluster/DOS:** N-streams-per-node = a mini-DOS on the venue (rate-limit/conn-cap/IP-ban) → the cluster = one venue × N risk-sharded nodes owns ONE venue connection. CURRENT: the data STREAM is already shared (one producer `Run.hpp:1275` fans to N); the market-state COMPUTE is NOT (each node recomputes — the unrealized "shared across cores" `PortfolioController.hpp:254` intent). So shared-market-state = part of the live-readiness FOUNDATION (multi-cluster per-node connections are unshippable). OVERSIGHTS: (a) sharing adds a cross-thread coherence surface = an SPMC problem (1 cluster-writer / N node-readers) → SPMC-via-SEQLOCK (non-blocking both ways, preserves per-node independence/H22) NOT SPMC-via-ring (a shared ring couples consumers — the slowest gates the producer — which is WHY ticks correctly stay N independent SPSC rings today); the existing slow→hot seqlock generalizes single-reader→multi-reader; UNIFIES with the engine→viewers snapshot publish (one seqlock-published-snapshot primitive serves both cluster→nodes market-state + engine→viewers) = E.1.3's aggregator-coherence job; (b) the DOS constraint applies to OUTBOUND order-submission too (N nodes → 1 venue order API → cluster must rate-coordinate; OMS submit-path + D-209 venue-net); (c) pinning best-effort (deployment dependency); (d) cluster computes at the fastest cadence (per-tick), nodes read per-poll; (e) all cache claims unmeasured. INVESTIGATE-autonomously vs DECIDE-with-operator split recorded. Homes: E.1.3 (coherent publish) / E.1.5 (per-cluster purity) / E.1.6 (per-cluster venue connection) / the optimization leaf / the H8 bench / the cache-budget tool.

<!-- D/C/F: D-231 -->
**D-231 — CAPTURED-not-chased (operator focus-check): the hot-path p99-variance investigation + the trade-branch-branchless candidate are the OPTIMIZATION thread → homed to the H8 bench (measure) + the optimization leaf (fix); NOT pursued now (can't honestly investigate an unmeasured cause).** Operator clarified the variance she's after is at **p99** (1-in-100, within the 256-tick window) — NOT the p9999 preemption tail. p99 at 1% ⇒ a RECURRING STRUCTURAL cause, two grounded ~1% candidates: (1) **param-update ticks** — every `poll_interval` (~100 → ~1%) the slow path pushes new params → next hot tick takes a `cached_seq` miss → the full `ParameterSlot_Read` seqlock retry (`ExecutionCore.hpp:309-321`) instead of the cheap cached read (a structural 1%-spike BY CONSTRUCTION); (2) **trade-decision ticks** — the rare event-push branch writes a `TradeEvent` to `event_ring` + a possible fresh-cache-line write-miss as the head advances. The **trade branch CAN be made branchless** (the DECISION already is mask-select `ExecutionCore.hpp:351-360`; the PUSH → H20 "always-write `event_ring[head]` + mask the head-ADVANCE by the trade flag"), **CORRECTED + ELEVATED** (operator; the critical-moment-determinism principle, now CODIFIED `critical-moment-determinism-over-average-latency.md` + memory): "rare+well-predicted=low cost" is the AVERAGE-case TRAP — the predictor trains not-taken, so the rare trade GUARANTEES a mispredict+flush landing EXACTLY on the trade-placement tick (jitter where capital is committed = the worst possible shape). The trade-branch is now a **LOCKED #1 H20 fix ON PRINCIPLE** (Task #15) — variance-at-the-critical-moment IS the cost, NOT average; branchless even when nominally slower; the H8 bench CONFIRMS the variance win + budget, it does NOT gate the decision. The PARAM-UPDATE spike (candidate 1) stays a measure-first item (its magnitude is genuinely unknown). The average-vs-jitter debate is CLOSED doctrine. FOCUS-DISCIPLINE (operator-raised "are we off track?"): YES this is the optimization thread, DOWNSTREAM of the instruments; chasing it now = the hand-waving we're eliminating. Homes: H8 bench (per-tick classification + perf-counter cache-miss) → the optimization leaf (branchless-push if measured to matter).

**RE-OPENED — the 3 the prior A⁺ missed:**

Single-threaded backtest = VACUOUS (no enable) AND structurally BLIND to H8's motivating failure class (false-sharing/cache-line-split need a 2nd core — `latency-path-discipline.md:358` history); the threaded synthetic `EngineSharded_Run`(BENCH=true) substrate IS faithful + enables sampling but is NOT CI-reproducible (no isolcpus/SCHED_FIFO on a shared runner → jitter false-fails; absolute-ns + hand-asserted floor-subtract + one-shot-GHz TSC→ns each corrupt it). ⟹ H8-as-a-hard-CI-gate may be the wrong tool; sound shape = **operator-run harness** (matches `latency-path-discipline.md:346`) + an optional **CI gross-catastrophe CYCLES-tripwire** on the backtest (catches 2-10× blowups, NOT the 500ns budget, samples-seen-asserted). **CAPTURED-PENDING operator ratification** (rec: A1+A2 harness+tripwire) — reshapes D-228.

<!-- D/C/F: D-233 -->
**D-233 — FORK A/B RESOLVED (operator 2026-06-16 s3): H8 ships as a STATIC ASM instruction-budget TOOL (hot + slow path), NOT a runtime latency bench. Instruction-count budgets replace wall-clock-ns — deterministic, trackable, zero engine behavior.** Operator: "make a tool to analyze the asm for instruction analysis [hot] and combine a slow path one as well… instruction count budgets instead of latency budgets, easier to track." **FORK A = the static axis (option 1):** disassemble the hot path (`ExecutionCore_Tick`/`BG_Evaluate`/`SG_Evaluate`) + slow path (`EventLoop_RebuildOneCore`/`RollingStats_Push`/`Regime_Classify`) → instruction-count (+ optional `llvm-mca` cycle-estimate) budgets via a manifest; assert no forbidden calls (malloc/free/__libc/lock) + count ≤ budget+tolerance. AUTOMATES the manual `latency-path-discipline.md` `-S` assembly-inspection checklist into a CI gate. **Option 2 (PMU `perf stat` — needs RUNNING the engine) = DEFERRED/PLANNED** (operator hasn't run it in a while → expects bitrot; its own running-validation effort; carries the runtime-bench concerns: the `ml_inference_ran` counter + ML-worst-case + the `CFG_DRIFT_STRICT_REFUSED` vacuity mask). **Option 3 (rdtsc wall-clock-ns) = DROPPED** (noisy/non-portable; the other two are better). **FORK B DISSOLVES:** the static tool changes ZERO engine behavior (it analyzes the compiled binary) → E.1.0 stays a true zero-behavior enforcement-scaffold IN-CHARTER; the counter/ML-worst-case/vacuity-mask were RUNTIME-bench solutions → they move to the option-2 running step (the static tool reads the ML path's compiled code directly → no run-vacuity). **PROCESS RIGHT-SIZED (supersedes D-232's scoped 3:3 re-confirm):** the tool is APPARATUS (low blast-radius, zero engine behavior, canonical sister to `check_struct_size_budget.py`) → proportionate path = BUILD + dogfood-iterate + teeth-proof, NOT a 3:3 adversarial gate (that fit the engine-adjacent runtime-bench shape, not a static analysis tool; `feedback_process_weight_by_surface_blast_radius`). Sister shape: manifest registry + `--selftest` teeth + TOOLS.md enrollment (D-137). UPGRADES `mechanical-verification-of-derived-code-facts.md`'s LATENCY+COMPLEXITY rows from runtime-benchmark → compile-time static gate (stronger tier). Design delta hot-vs-slow: hot = straight-line → single instruction-count budget; slow = has loops → function-body instruction budget + loop-STRUCTURE/complexity check (catches O(W) regressions; enforces H11 constant-iter) + no-forbidden-calls. An instruction-count RATCHET-down is feasible at E.1.0 (deterministic, unlike a wall-clock ratchet) with a pinned toolchain + tolerance; E.1.1's rename proves instruction-neutral under it.
