**E.1.1 IS more than a rename (operator correct, VERIFIED):** E.1.1 = mechanical rename + cfg-key CLEAN-BREAK (parser HARD-REFUSES legacy `core_*` keys — its frontmatter: "the only behavior change") + `single_core` LIVE deletion (D-61), across a ~590-file surface (351+217+22). The HOT/SLOW paths stay names-only → the analyzer proves hot-path NEUTRALITY across the churn; the cfg-break + deletion are off-hot-path (boot parser + deprecated path) → guarded by the determinism gate + tests, not the instruction analyzer. ⟹ stand the FULL conformance net up in E.1.0 BEFORE E.1.1 to net the mechanical storm.
**THE RESHAPE — not an instruction counter but a CONFORMANCE ANALYZER = `latency-path-discipline.md` + the hot/slow Hard Invariants, MECHANIZED from disassembly.** Manifest-driven CHECK-REGISTRY (each check = 1 row) over hot (`ExecutionCore_Tick`/`BG_Evaluate`/`SG_Evaluate`) + slow (`EventLoop_RebuildOneCore`/`RollingStats_Push`/`Regime_Classify`), on the PRODUCTION build (NO `LATENCY_PROFILING` — gate the SHIPPED hot path, not the sampling-instrumented one). Check-set (each decidable from ASM, maps to an invariant): instruction-count budget (the latency table, executable) · **branch-count = the REAL H7/H20 RED gate** (the "stronger non-grep detector" the plan DEFERRED — ASM branches are ground truth; KNOWN_COHORT shrink-allowlist for loop-bounds/`__builtin_expect`; SUBSUMES task #2's grep → keep grep only as optional source-locator) · no-forbidden-calls malloc/free/__libc/lock (H1/Rule 2) · no-stack-spills (checklist item 2) · **no scalar-float (H4)** · no `div`/`idiv` (§5 reciprocals) · **no indirect-call/vtable (H2)** · inlining + AVX-512-emitted via g++ `-fopt-info` (compiler-remark axis; GCC-only, verified) · slow-path loop-STRUCTURE/complexity (H11; closes the stale `O(W)` doc-debt). Producer-fan-out/OMS-drainer/parser = cheap 1-row follow-ons (scaled by cadence).
**COMPLETENESS-FIXES folded (4-pillar self-audit):** (1) **the analyzer ASSERTS its OWN non-vacuity** (symbol found + real inlined body disassembled + count>0) — else it's the Class-51 vacuously-green guard it codifies against (eat-own-dogfood; mirror check_h10's AVX-defined assert) — THE headline overlooked item; (2) re-baseline mode (`--update-budgets`) + tolerance + pinned toolchain (counts shift w/ g++ version/inlining — like the determinism golden); (3) HONEST scope: slow-path static = body-count + loop-structure, NOT dynamic per-cycle (that's option-2/running); a no-REGRESSION ratchet, not an optimizer; (4) manifest cross-checks/sources `tools/lib/sharded_files.txt` (no 3rd Class-18 mirror) + JOINS E.1.1's rename-sweep (its symbol/file refs + the tool's own Core→Node rename; the analyzer IS E.1.1's hot-path proof-of-neutrality); (5) NO existing ASM tool (verified — green). Built as the 2nd instance of the derived-fact-budget-gate framework (shared scaffold w/ check_struct_size_budget.py if ~0 marginal cost; else Stage-2 the meta-pattern).
**DESIGN_SPECS:** NEW static-latency-path-conformance pattern + AMEND mechanical-verification-of-derived-code-facts.md (LATENCY+COMPLEXITY → compile-time tier) + cross-ref H2/H4/H7/H11/H20 + latency-path-discipline.md as "mechanically enforced by ⟨analyzer⟩".

**D-235 — E-series GOAL (operator 2026-06-16 s3): drive the slow path (and hot path) toward ZERO DATA-DEPENDENT branches; the E.1.0 conformance analyzer's branch-classifier is the PROGRESS METER. E.1.0 builds the meter, does NOT reduce; the reduction homes to the optimization leaf (slow) + #15 (hot).** Operator: "ideally were going to reduce this as part of E, id rather have zero branches on the slow path, but its fine for this specific ship." **PRECISION:** literally-zero is impossible (a loop needs a back-edge); the achievable target is **zero DATA-DEPENDENT branches** (loop back-edges + `__builtin_expect`-rare + `if constexpr` stay — predictable/~free) = the **H20 ideal** (mask-blend dispatch) + **H11** (constant-iter loops). The analyzer (D-234) classifies branches {compile-time-gone / loop-backedge / rare-event-cold / **data-dependent-warm**} → the data-dependent-warm count is the number to drive to 0 (ties to critical-moment-determinism: those ARE the jitter-at-the-critical-moment). The per-branch breakdown (source-line + best-effort "why") becomes the optimization ROADMAP. **HOMES:** the slow-path branchless/compute leaf (D-229/#9 "own .E leaf, E.1.M-class", tracked in the `.E.1` sub-master) gets the slow-path reduction; **#15** (trade-branch H20) is the hot-path instance. Fold into the optimization-leaf body when that leaf is scaffolded.

The post-D-236 hardened re-audit (R-I, this session) found the slow ENTRYPOINT `EventLoop_RebuildOneCore` carries **1645 floats + 154 divs that are ALL H4-EXEMPT** (feature-domain transcendentals sqrt/exp/pow @ RidgeBlender/ConfidenceScore/BarrierGate/FeatureStandardizer + `Money_ToDouble` display @ FixedPointN.hpp:1890; the certified `Money_Div` is branchless `udiv256_qr`, never hw idiv) — so D-236's "HARD invariants float/div gate now" would FALSE-RED the slow entrypoint. The structural resolution = an exemption mechanism, best built ONCE now (design-once-maintain-forever; a 2-row meter or a fuzzy grandfathered count is a weaker foundation a later leaf must RE-TRAVERSE — and E.1.2's relayout TOUCHES RebuildOneCore, so the net MUST watch it). **THE DESIGN (B):** (1) **4-row manifest** — ExecutionCore_Tick (hot) + RollingStats_Push (slow leaf) + EventLoop_RebuildOneCore (slow ENTRYPOINT, .isra-reached) + Regime_Classify (slow); BG/SG covered transitively (inlined copies — the standalone GateParameters copies are a Class-18 mirror, separate finding). (2) **EXTEND the existing branch-allowlist pattern to a per-row SOURCE-keyed `allow` list** {src_pattern: rationale} covering float/div/forbidden alike — file-level for pure-feature files (RidgeBlender/ConfidenceScore/BarrierGate/FeatureStandardizer), file:line for the mixed FixedPointN.hpp (so a NEW money-float there still REDs); a HARD signal whose `-l` source is NOT allowlisted REDs. ONE allowlist concept for branches + floats + divs + the FINDING-A pthread (Notify.hpp source-allow, cold-kill-edge rationale, D-238-candidate). (3) **teeth**: an allow-source signal passes; a NON-allowed-source signal REDs (the precision proof). (4) a final **verify pass** (the exemption is itself a false-green-risk surface — Class-51 — so it earns one). **WHAT D-236 STILL HOLDS:** the BRANCH curation + reduction (warmup-why per branch / drive-to-0 / bandit-overlap) STAYS the optimization leaf — D-237 expands ONLY the float/div-exemption + manifest-coverage axis (HARD-invariant gating), NOT the branch-reduction axis. data-dependent-warm branches stay grandfathered (D-236). **Net gate model: HARD malloc/indirect/real-lock gate absolutely; float/div gate against the source-`allow` list; branches ratchet (grandfathered).**

FORBIDDEN-calls exempt by file:LINE ONLY (never file-level — the final-verify latent-hole close: a file-level allow must not blanket-mask a future malloc/stdio).

- **FINDING A (H3 pthread on slow rebuild) = cold/sanctioned** (TECH_DEBT-205): edge-triggered kill-trip Notify, sanctioned mechanism; not a steady-state violation.

<!-- D/C/F: D-239 -->
**D-239 — E.1.1 dive resolutions (gate-amended): ExecutionCore PRESERVE · single_core CLEAN-DELETE (no tombstone) · gate-scope `_PER_CORE`→`_PER_NODE` · file-renames in-scope.** The E.1.1 dive (C-1 re-ground → 7-agent `/precoding-audit-gate` RED→amended) resolved the open scope/naming questions D-27/D-61 left:
- **ExecutionCore PRESERVE** (resolves D-27's silence on it): `ExecutionCore`/`ExecutionCore_Tick`/`CoreFrameworks`/`MULTICORE` + CPU-hardware `core` are NOT renamed by Core→Node — they name the per-tick execution ENGINE / the dir / the hardware, distinct from the node/shard (the pinning surface uses `cpu_id`/`CPU`/`SMT`, lexically disjoint from the `core_`/`Core*` shard tokens; renaming `ExecutionCore` would break the conformance kernel manifest). **Disambiguation rule:** `core_*`/`Core*` shard ids → node; "core" = CPU hardware → stays core. Premise: a NODE owns **2 CPUs** (hot+slow thread pair), `node_id ≠ cpu_id` (operator: "each node uses 2 cores = semantic diff"). DESIGN_PHILOSOPHY §15 glossary RATIFIED (was PROPOSED/NOT-YET) + per-node-purity-scale-invariance.md sentence sharpened.
- **② single_core CLEAN-DELETE, NO tombstone** (refines D-61): `engine_mode` verified NOT persisted/wire → full clean delete (both `ENGINE_MODE_*` enums + the field + the legacy path); the operator cfg KEYS HARD-REFUSE (loud, NOT silent-ignore, NOT alias); ZERO tombstones (operator anti-bricked-bits preference — generalized into the reclaim-not-freeze memory). 3-phase leaves-first.
- **③ gate-scope `_PER_CORE`→`_PER_NODE`** (consistency — the scope IS the per-node scope, mirror of `_GLOBAL`) + **① file-basename renames IN-SCOPE** (the 6 `Core*` files + ~27 `#include` cascade, folded into the TD-175 cascade tool).
Full detail: the E.1.1 plan body §"Gate synthesis — amendments + corrected dive scope" (the dive SSoT).

<!-- D/C/F: D-241 -->
**D-241 — manual TP/SL adjustment in the decoupled world: a `fox-cli` UDS verb (keep) vs dropped (autonomous-only) — OPEN, decide at `.E.2`.** Surfaced 2026-06-21 (operator design Q: "when we decouple GUI+engine, will adjustable TP/SL work?"). Grounded current state: the GUI-drag TP/SL is ALREADY inert on the sharded path (**TD-184** — the drag writes `pos->take_profit_price` [`Async.hpp:246-247`, the *display* level] but the hot exit gate fires on `core->live_tp`/`live_sl` [`ExecutionCore.hpp:337-338`/`:427`]; the sharded path never copies it → a Class-2 display↔execution lie, gates live). `.E.2` HARD-DEPRECATES the GUI (**D-26** → `engine_gui` archived to `legacy/`; viewers become read-only `fox-tui`) → **the drag affordance disappears regardless**; control moves to the `fox-cli` UDS command channel (**D-52** verb set). So the decision is whether manual exit-adjust survives as a verb:
- **Option A — autonomous engine, no manual exit-adjust.** TP/SL fully engine-managed (strategy + ratchet + regime); no exit-adjust verb; TD-184 resolves by REMOVING the (inert, dangerous) affordance. Simplest/safest; matches the headless-autonomous-shard-farm direction.
- **Option B — keep it as a `fox-cli` exit-adjust verb over UDS.** The verb's engine-side application = the TD-184 wiring (write `core->live_tp`/`live_sl` on the per-node exit path + re-anchor the ratchet) → TD-184 (.E.1) and the .E.2 verb are ONE piece of work, command-triggered not drag-triggered. Entangles with **TD-189** (converge the live-multiplier storage first) + the `.E.2` command safety-constraint model (validate-before-apply, like "no strategy-change with open positions").
Governs: **TD-184** (.E.1 — wire-vs-remove) + the **`.E.2` D-52 fox-cli verb set** (include exit-adjust or not). Lean (my read; operator's call): **A** fits the autonomous direction; **B** is clean given the UDS command pattern *if* a human override on live positions is wanted. Either way the CURRENT state (an affordance that looks live + does nothing) must not survive to live.
<!-- STATUS: OPEN — decide at `.E.2` (decoupling). Governs TD-184 (.E.1 in-process fix: wire-or-remove) + the .E.2 D-52 verb set. Homed: .E.2 plan (command-channel/verb-set §) + the decoupling endgoal roadmap + the TD-184 ledger cross-ref. -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-242 -->
**D-242 — ③ cfg validation = a "config compiler": a verify-pass that collects ALL problems as ERRORS vs WARNINGS (compiler-style readout) + a clean compile GATES the run; HARD on fresh starts, WARN-keep-old on hot-reload; key-match-based.** The HOW of D-219/D-223 (clean-break + hard-refuse-not-alias) — reaffirmed + ELEVATED 2026-06-21 at pickup (8-agent pre-coding cascade, SSoT `plan_checks/2026-06-21-E.1.1-cfg-clean-break-precoding-cascade.md`) after the operator re-examined the refuse premise + reframed it as a "config compiler."
- **The model (operator, 2026-06-21): the cfg "compiles."** One verify pass collects EVERY problem in the file (line# + reason + migration hint), prints a COMPILER-STYLE readout, and a clean compile GATES handoff to the run — one-pass fixing, NOT stop-on-first-error whack-a-mole. This is the FIRST-CANONICAL SEED of E.2's planned `hierarchical-config-validation-pattern`.
- **ERRORS (block the run) vs WARNINGS (proceed, but listed)** — resolves the values question. ERRORS = unknown key · bad node index · UNPARSEABLE value (`risk_pct=banana`). WARNINGS = in-range-clampable value (the EXISTING clamp-and-warn behavior, just routed into the same readout) · deprecated-but-recognized key. ③ ships the config-compiler FRAMEWORK + the full KEY/INDEX error set (its charter; cheap — the parse already detects these); the systematic SEMANTIC value-validation sweep (routing every value site into the readout) = E.2's extension into the same structure.
- **Compile-gates-run, UNIFIED for fresh starts (refines the earlier per-cadence WARN/abort, D1).** A fresh start — live boot AND backtest — requires a clean compile (no ERRORS) to proceed, not just live. Rationale: a backtest that silently defaults a typo'd key produces MISLEADING results (you evaluate settings you never set); the one-pass readout makes the fix ~free, so the safety is near-zero-friction. Hot-reload (already running) + GUI can't "fail to start" → validate → WARN + keep-old (the only genuinely-special cadences). **OUTPUT (refined 2026-06-22, decoupling-aligned): a terminal readout + a `config_error_log` file — NOT a GUI banner** (the GUI hard-deprecates at E.2 per D-26; terminal+file is headless-aligned, persisted/recallable [`cat` it after a failed boot], and surface-agnostic — works for the CLI backtest too). Result/flag-based, NEVER `exit()` inside `ControllerConfig_Load` (kills a running engine on a hot-reload typo + crashes the GUI's `Settings_Load:887` call; 3 independent cascade confirms).
- **Mechanism: KEY-MATCH-based fault** (not field-written — else a clamped/defaulted value over-fires); aligns with the sibling precedent `ConfigField_Set` (already returns 0 on an unknown key, tested `controller_test.cpp:18376`). Fixed-buffer readout (H1 no-heap; cap ~32 + "…and N more").

Backtest compile-GATED on ERRORS too — CONFIRMED 2026-06-21 (operator: "yeah clean compile"); SUPERSEDES the earlier D1 backtest-warn-and-run. A clean compile now gates EVERY fresh start (live + backtest); only hot-reload + GUI warn-keep-old (can't fail-to-start a running engine). No open refinements remain on D-242. [AMENDED 2026-06-22 → D-247: the adversarial re-gate REOPENED one. D-242's own ERROR list (body above) includes "UNPARSEABLE value (`risk_pct=banana`)", but ③'s KEY/INDEX-only scope + the per-node parser DISCARDING `Money_FromString().flags` (`ControllerConfig.hpp:2850`, `strstr`-matched, never clamped) mean ③ as designed does NOT deliver value-errors on the per-node CAPITAL path — the founding stop-loss-typo case stays OPEN. Scope decision (A-narrow: fold the 4 capital fields' value-validation + the exact-suffix matcher into ③ / vs B: defer all value-validation to E.2) OPEN at D-247.] Remaining ③ calls (F2 post-loop idx-validation / F4 parser⊆registry guard + walker@.F.4e / F5 Cores→Nodes terminology + tests / fgets[256] harden / GUI warn-banner) tracked in the plan_checks SSoT; adversarial re-gate owed on the locked scope before coding. -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-243 -->
**D-243 — config path handling: ③ does the buffer-robustness fix ONLY; the global deployment-root (relative paths feeding models/logs/data/everything) → E.2.** Sub-thread of D-242 (2026-06-21, operator-steered). The ③ config-compiler must read long lines correctly — a deep absolute `node_N_model_dir=<path>` overflows the `char line[256]` parse buffer; the overflow tail, if it contains `=` (common in ML dir names like `fold=3`), becomes a bogus key → a SPURIOUS compile-error on a valid config (and silently truncates the path today → wrong model dir loaded).
- **③ fix (buffer robustness, IN-scope):** size the line buffer to fit a real max line (~320, ≥ key + the 256-char path-value storage) + on a genuine overflow emit a clean ERROR (`line N: too long`) instead of silently truncating. Fixes the spurious-error AND the pre-existing silent-truncation. Low error-surface; carries its own tests.
- **Operator's better idea — a SINGLE global deployment ROOT** feeding ALL file I/O (models + logs + data + metrics + the config tree), relative-path-resolved against it, machine-portable DERIVED default (cfg-setting → `$FOXML_*` env → build-time `<repo>/...` default → `.exists()` guard; per `feedback_machine_portable_resolver_for_committed_tool_paths`). Backward-compatible (absolute paths resolve as-is).
- **→ E.2, NOT ③.** (1) It's a DEPLOYMENT concept — the root for a headless service running from `/opt/...` is E.2's to define (E.2 owns the config-file structure + deployment boundary); a dev-only `<repo>/models` default now would just be re-derived at E.2. (2) **A root SETTING added in ③ with no consumer yet = an orphan cfg flag (Class-44) — the EXACT anti-pattern ③'s clean-break fights**; the root + every file-I/O site that resolves against it must land together (E.2). ③'s config-compiler is the forward-compatible SEED (it already validates the relative keys; E.2 adds the resolver). Captured here (D-243); the E.2-subplan-body fold happens at E.2's dive (editing E.2's v0.1 draft now trips B-Plus on its pre-existing illustrative-code blocks — a known draft condition, not this decision's).
<!-- STATUS: DECIDED 2026-06-21 (operator: "yeah, log this decision"; the global-root generalization is the operator's — it's WHY the root work relocates to E.2 cleanly vs a model-only slice in ③). ③ = config-compiler + buffer fix (D-242 + this). Global deployment root → E.2 (captured in D-243; E.2-body fold deferred to E.2's dive). Does NOT touch the model stamp (path = load-location, not model identity; verify at code-time). -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-244 -->
**D-244 — backtest surface in the decoupled world: a CLI runner (`foxml-train` / `foxmlsuite --backtest --config X`) + an OPTIONAL read-only viewer, NOT a GUI utility — OPEN, decide at `.E.2`.** Operator design Q 2026-06-22 (sub-thread of D-242's config-compiler output): backtest is decoupled from LIVE (research, not the 24/7 capital engine) AND the GUI hard-deprecates at `.E.2` (D-26) → should backtest stay a GUI utility or become CLI? Apply the SAME decoupling pattern as live (headless compute + read-only viewer + CLI control):
- **CLI is the right core** (E.2's already-planned `foxml-train`, replacing the foxml_suite GUI; operator sketch `./foxmlsuite --backtest --config path`). Wins (operator-named): SSH-friendly (no display forwarding) · scriptable/batchable (grids, cron, CI) · **reproducible — `--config path` makes the exact run-settings a saved, versioned, re-runnable FILE** (a click-knobs GUI never captures "what did I run") · lightweight (no SDL2/ImGui deps).
- **GUI-viewer-nice isn't lost — it stops being the RUNNER.** A read-only viewer attaches to the backtest's OUTPUT ARTIFACTS (equity curve / trades / metrics) for visual exploration, WITHOUT the backtest logic living in the GUI. Not either/or: CLI runs (reproducible/headless), a viewer reads results (visual) — the headless-engine + read-only-viewer split, applied to backtest.
- **③ is unaffected + correct either way:** the D-242 config-compiler OUTPUT (terminal + `config_error_log`) is SURFACE-AGNOSTIC — right whether backtest runs in the GUI suite today or the CLI tomorrow.
<!-- STATUS: OPEN — decide at `.E.2` (the `foxml-train` CLI / GUI-deprecation ship). Operator lean (musing, not locked): CLI core for reproducibility + SSH, optional viewer on artifacts. Captured here + breadcrumbed in the decoupling roadmap; folds into the `.E.2` plan body at its dive. Sibling of D-241 — both are the `.E.2` runtime/viewer-split surface (D-241 = control verbs; D-244 = the backtest tool's surface). -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-247 -->
**D-247 — ③ config-compiler adversarial re-gate (2026-06-22): the FRAMEWORK is validated, but ③ as scoped does NOT close the silent-wrong-capital VALUE class it was reframed (D-242) to close — scope decision OPEN (A-narrow vs B).** A targeted 5-agent a-class re-gate (one per config-compiler delta) on the LOCKED ③ scope. SSoT: `plan_checks/2026-06-22-E.1.1-config-compiler-adversarial-regate.md`. All 5 PARTIAL-REFUTED — the framework (collect-all · flag-based · never-abort-in-Load · compile-gates-every-fresh-start · the uint32 bitmap gate · COMPLETE caller enumeration) HOLDS; 3 HIGH change scope (all orchestrator-code-verified at HEAD `1da1c1c`):
- **HIGH (capital, F-A) — the per-node parser is the real surface, and ③ doesn't fix it.** Two parsers, opposite semantics: the registry path (`CfgFieldDispatch.hpp:99-102`) clamps+flags; the per-node hand-rolled path (`ControllerConfig.hpp:2850`) uses `strstr` substring-match, DISCARDS `Money_FromString().flags`, NEVER clamps. Verified: `node_0_risk_pct=999`→999% live alloc (`Run.hpp:924`); `node_0_max_drawdown_pct=999`→kill-switch silently dead; `node_0_stop_loss_pct=banana`→SL silently disabled (THE founding stop-loss-typo case); `node_0_risk_pct_typo`→`strstr`-accepted→writes slot 0. ③'s key-match (value-validation deferred to E.2 per D-243) closes silent-wrong-KEY, NOT silent-wrong-VALUE — so D-242:body's "UNPARSEABLE value → ERROR" is unmet for the per-node capital path.
- **HIGH (determinism, F-B) — the fault field is a fingerprint surface.** `ControllerConfig` is SHA-256'd RAW into the model lineage (`ControllerConfig.hpp:347` + `Fingerprint.hpp:180`); "not wire" is false (H9/H12). FIX (mandatory, EITHER option): struct keeps only a 4-byte gate-summary guaranteed-0-on-clean-load (or fingerprint-excluded); the readout list is stack-local in `Load`, not a struct member; golden-regen owed. → folds into E.1.2 (struct-finalize + the MISSING `ControllerConfig` size-pin, F-I).
- **HIGH (design-consistency, F-C) — `config_error_log` file contradicts D-243.** Zero code refs, no root resolver = the Class-44 path-orphan D-243 relocated to E.2; `main.cpp:88` already redirects stderr→`logging/engine.log`. FIX: ③ = terminal/stderr-only; DEFER the file to E.2 with the deployment-root cohort.
- **MED:** readout ERROR-prioritization so a capital ERROR can't be truncated behind cosmetic warnings (F-D); the DEAD EngineTUI hot-reload edit-site → drop + delete the `TUI_HandleInput`/`PortfolioController_HotReload` orphan pair (F-E, verified 0-callers); consumer-wiring incl. the Async copy-order trap + a shared `cfg_compile_ok()` helper (F-F/F-G); List-C test must use a substring-colliding typo (F-H).

**THE FOUNDATIONAL SCOPE DECISION (why this is operator-grade):** ③ is not just an E.1.1 deliverable — it is the cfg-validation FOUNDATION the E.1 capital superstructure (E.1.3 coherence / E.1.4 fill / E.1.5 purity) + the multi-exchange framework (E.1.6) + the headless config system (E.2, where ③ is the named `hierarchical-config-validation-pattern` SEED) all build on. The E.1 cluster discipline is foundation-by-layer THEN superstructure-by-spine: the foundation must be SOUND before the spines build on it. Per-node CAPITAL value-validation is a foundation concern — E.1.5's A34 purity governor + the HARD live-enable preconditions (E.1.3 ∧ E.1.5) operate on these per-node capital values. Three positions:
- **B (key/index-only, defer all):** ③ exactly as designed; matcher + ALL value-validation → E.2. Founding motivation unmet; E.1.5 governs on unvalidated per-node capital inputs; E.1.6 piles more per-node fields onto the broken `strstr` matcher; backtest produces misleading per-node-capital numbers.
- **A-narrow (RECOMMENDED — close the CAPITAL class now):** ③ adds (1) the exact-suffix matcher (fold the 7 `strstr` branches into the `:2929` exact-match dispatch — closes the key under-fire, ~mechanical, zero latency cost) + (2) value-validation for the 4 CAPITAL fields only (`risk_pct`/`max_drawdown_pct`/`take_profit_pct`/`stop_loss_pct`: `Money_FromString().flags`→ERROR + sane upper bound→ERROR). Delivers the founding motivation; sets E.1.5's purity on validated inputs; gives E.2 a proven pattern to extend to the NON-capital fields. The full semantic value-sweep (all fields/ranges/deprecation) STAYS E.2.
- **A-broad (overscope):** ③ does the whole value-sweep for ALL fields now — pulls E.2's work forward, balloons ③; NOT recommended (overengineering boundary; non-capital fields aren't foundation-critical).

Disciplines favoring A-narrow: heavier-audit-posture-for-capital (burden on REMOVING a control) · phased-pre-rework-correctness-foundation (harden the foundation before the spines) · no-defer-for-effort · opportunistic-tech-debt-closure (~0 marginal cost — the matcher fold is cheap) · the operator's own founding motivation. Honest caveat: the capital spines are LIVE-OFF until v5.16 (D-168), so Option B is not live-unsafe NOW — but it (a) makes backtest per-node-capital numbers untrustworthy, (b) builds the spines on unvalidated inputs, (c) does the matcher fix LATER anyway, after E.1.6 has added more fields to the broken parser.

<!-- D/C/F: D-248 -->
**D-248 — ③ capital value-validation: forward-compat-corrected design (asymmetric caps via the `barrier_is_corrupt` SSoT; per-field bounds; scope = ~12 capital/exit fields; ERROR-block; hand-rolled inline).** Supersedes D-247's "A-narrow inline-4 + uniform >100→ERROR" after the full I→A cascade (SSoT `plan_checks/2026-06-22-E.1.1-config-compiler-IA-cascade-synthesis.md`) + an operator-directed forward-compat sweep against the future E-series ("design with the future in mind, strong foundation"). Operator-decided 2026-06-22.
- **Asymmetric caps — NO MARGIN** ([[project_no_margin_trading]]). Loss/sizing side ≤ 100% (`stop_loss_pct`/`risk_pct`/`max_drawdown_pct` — can't lose more than the amount bought / the node's risk allocation; `>100%` = margin = ERROR). Gain side generous: `take_profit_pct` (+ per-strategy `*_tp_pct`) ≤ ~1000% (a >100% TP target is rare-but-legit; gains aren't margin-bounded). A uniform `>100→ERROR` was WRONG — it rejects a legit >100% TP AND contradicts the operator's OWN shipped SSoT.
- **Reuse `tt::barrier_is_corrupt`** (`ML_Headers/BarrierValidation.hpp`, D-221: `BARRIER_SANE_MAX_SL=1.0`, `BARRIER_SANE_MAX_TP=10.0`) for the tp/sl bounds — the canonical sister, applied today at both train (`StampHelper.hpp:258`) + serve (`NodeModelZoo.hpp:1138`) seams (single-source; never a re-stated literal). Exact-TP-cap refine = TECH_DEBT-199.
- **Per-field bounds, NOT a uniform constant.** The cohort spans [0,100%]/[0,1000%]/[0,1]/[1,20]/[0,10]-mult; E.1.6 adds enum-kind. ③ reads per-field bounds (barrier_is_corrupt for tp/sl; the descriptor's clamp_min/max VALUES — sound, distinct from the vacuous clamp MECHANISM the cascade refuted) → extensible to every future per-node field (the D-206 ratchet knobs + E.1.6 mode/exchange enums) by construction.
- **Scope = ~12** (operator-confirmed): the 4 core + the 8 per-strategy tp/sl overrides (ml/simpledip/mr/emacross `_tp_pct`/`_sl_pct`) — same semantics, exit-capital-bearing, the surface the D-206 ratchet re-opens. The multiplier/fraction outliers (`tp2_mult`/`partial_exit_pct`/`fee_floor_mult`) get their own per-field bounds if folded, else → E.2 value-sweep.
- **Hand-rolled inline, ERROR-block, sentinel-aware** (`0`=inherit VALID), at the 2 parser idioms (dedicated branches `:2847/:2858` + `_PARSE_OV_PCT` `:2939`). NOT via `tt::cfg_parse_field` (vacuous clamp + the override route destroys the `0`=inherit sentinel). + convert all 7 strstr→exact-match. + atof→parse_double_fast. Full edit-set in the IA-cascade SSoT.
- **FORWARD-COMPAT (sweep-verified):** D-206 SL/TP ratchet/give-back redesign (`subplans/2026-06-12-…E-exit-system-trailing-redesign.md`, captured-parked) does NOT conflict — downstream of the cfg seed (cfg tp/sl stay target/floor; trail distance is a separate `sl_trail_mult`); it adds NEW per-node knobs as auto-flowing `core_N_*` overrides → ③'s per-field framework validates them by construction. The **Σ≤100% no-margin allocation-sum** (Σ node_risk_pct ≤ 100%; node_risk_pct = fraction of TOTAL balance, `Run.hpp:924`) is CROSS-NODE, NOT ③'s per-field job → **TECH_DEBT-211 → E.5** (bound tightens to `1−reserve_pct`); ③ carries a forward-seam disclaimer.
- **Codification slate** (candidates; full Classes/specs at ③ ship-close per pattern-codification-lifecycle): NEW anti-pattern "uniform-bound validation over a heterogeneous field cohort" (the TP-ceiling bug + `partial_exit_pct=0` collision) · the vacuous-percent-clamp (codebase-wide; fold into Class 41 or new) · DESIGN_SPEC `hierarchical-config-validation-pattern` (③ = first-canonical seed; E.2 promotes) + extend `capital-allocation-policy-pattern` for the Σ no-margin check.

Re-audit subagents to be re-run on THIS corrected shape (operator-directed) before coding. SSoT: plan_checks/2026-06-22-E.1.1-config-compiler-IA-cascade-synthesis.md. -->

**D-258 — Clean-break unknown-key HARD-REFUSE SCOPED to the parser-EXCLUSIVE core_*/node_* namespace; the GLOBAL-unknown refuse DEFERRED to the multi-parser unification (2026-06-24).** SSoT: ControllerConfig.hpp loop-tail (~:3134) + the false-refuse-safety test control.
- **The hazard:** TWO boot parsers read the SAME cfg file (BinanceConfig_Load + ControllerConfig_Load, main.cpp:75/76). A naive "refuse ALL unknown keys" FALSE-REFUSES a sibling parser's key (use_testnet is BinanceConfig's) → breaks boot. So the refuse is scoped to the namespace ControllerConfig EXCLUSIVELY owns (core_*/node_*) — verified exclusive (the per-node block is the sole node_ handler; no core_ handler post-rename; BinanceConfig owns zero core_/node_).
- **core_*** → fault + the .E.1.1 migration message; **node_*** unrecognized (typo / idx≥16 / retired) → fault. Rides the existing cfg_compile_ok gate. The broader global-unknown refuse needs N1's unification (the global key-set becomes explicit) → HOMED (#10).
- **Test non-vacuity = the false-refuse-safety control:** a foreign BinanceConfig key (use_testnet) is NOT refused by ControllerConfig's clean-break (else boot breaks).

<!-- D/C/F: D-259 -->
**D-259 — Fault-bit taxonomy: a DISTINCT cfg_load_fault_flags bit per fault CLASS; NEVER merge two severities into one bit (Class-49 signal-merge) (2026-06-24).** SSoT: CfgFieldRegistry.hpp:224-227.
- CFG_FAULT_CAPITAL_MALFORMED (0) / CAPITAL_OUT_OF_RANGE (1) / UNKNOWN_KEY (2) / FEATURE_MALFORMED (3). A malformed CAPITAL value (always-fatal, all modes, no-margin D2) and a malformed FEATURE (determinism-bearing, a policy call on refuse-vs-warn) must stay SEPARABLE → distinct bits. uint32 has 28 free bits (the widen pressure is on the separate metadata uint16, not this bitmap).

So `risk_pct=999` set globally + nodes inheriting (the most common config) → 999% allocation, MISSED. The original cascade's A-B pass called this "subsumed by the merge" — a premature subsumption (conflated "the override now lives in nodes[c]" with "nodes[c] IS the value the consumer reads"; false on the global-fallback path). 4-way confirmed (i+a+c-class + orchestrator code-read of Async.hpp:347-359 / ControllerEventLoop.hpp:2920-2924). **Converged build (LANDED, controller_test 3690/0; engine `ControllerConfig.hpp`+99 / `CfgFieldRegistry.hpp`+7 / `BacktestEngine.hpp`+15):** (1) Money-native `capital_value_out_of_range(v,cap)` importing `tt::BARRIER_SANE_MAX_SL/TP`, decoupled from `barrier_is_corrupt` (D-266); (2) per-node sweep = if-constexpr walk over `FOREACH_PER_NODE_CFG_FIELD` reading `nodes[c]` (NOT the handoff's "via the masks" — no FIELD_IDX→nodes[c] accessor exists; mirrors `EMIT_PER_NODE_COPY`); (3) **global-flat leg** for risk_pct/max_drawdown_pct driven OFF `FOREACH_PER_NODE_ARRAY_OVERRIDE` (Option A, registry-driven → AUTO-DISSOLVES when E.1.2 deletes the arrays; also closes F2's stale-`nodes[c]` since it reads the fresh flat); (4) dollar floors + `min_kill_loss<starting_balance`; (5) the **F3 exhaustiveness tripwire** (`ALL_CAPITAL_BOUND_VARIANTS` vs `CAPITAL_BOUND_SWEEP_HANDLED` per-field static_assert — a future E.1.6 variant with no sweep branch = COMPILE ERROR; vacuous now); (6) **F2** optimizer `config_override` gated by a range-ENDPOINT probe (refuse-don't-coerce). <!-- D/C/F: D-278 -->
**D-278 — design principle: DELETABLE-BY-CONSTRUCTION internally; tombstone ONLY the irreducible wire/persistence surface (operator-articulated, 2026-06-28).** Operator directive: prefer designs where deleting a "part" cleanly cascades through every consumer (registry-driven / compiler-enforced) over designs that accrete tombstones. **Boundary (COMPOSES with H21, does NOT override it):** H21 tombstoning is FORCED by external visibility — a persisted snapshot VERSION, a persisted/wire enum CODE, an HMAC body field, an operator's cfg key — because old state / un-updated nodes carry the old meaning (Knight Capital), so those physically CANNOT be clean-deleted. The principle: make everything BEHIND the wire/persist line registry-cascaded + freely deletable, and keep that line as SMALL + explicit as possible (minimize what ever needs a tombstone). **First canonical:** item-4's global-flat leg rides `FOREACH_PER_NODE_ARRAY_OVERRIDE` (an internal, non-persisted registry) → when E.1.2 deletes the legacy arrays the global-flat checks vanish FOR FREE (the cascade goes the GOOD direction; zero hand-cleanup). Codified: memory `feedback_prefer_deletable_cascade_over_tombstone` + a design-time complement section in `dead-code-and-identifier-retirement-discipline.md`.
<!-- STATUS: DECIDED 2026-06-28 — design default going forward; composes with H21 (which governs the irreducible external surface). First applied in item-4's registry-driven global-flat leg. -->

BYTE-NEUTRAL (defaults 0 = current pad → no wire change / no version bump for the carve) + WIDEN-LATER-SAFE (widening into reserved-zero bits is byte-safe if distributed ever happens). Lands E.1.2 (honors E.1.3's inbound seam). NOT designed for cross-server/distributed (operator: not building it, no hardware to test).

- **D1 — persist-layout HASH as the primary forcing function.** `constexpr` hash of `(name,type,wire-order)` over the FULL persist walk (flat + delegates) + `static_assert(hash==EXPECTED_V11)`. Forces a version bump on ANY add/drop/reorder/size-neutral-swap — the only guard non-vacuous against E.1.2's size+count-canceling delta (drop node_dd_pct −16B/−1row + add partner_pending +16B/+1row = net 0). - **D2 — UNIFY the 3 parallel NodeCtx registries → ONE `FOREACH_NODE_CTX_FIELD`** (init/reset/persist views generated from one; OMS-model; `{persist-kind, storage-kind, commit-kind, wire-order}` columns). Rationale: design-once (a later unify would re-traverse the wire code); the 3rd-parallel-registry is the SSoT pull-signal.
- **D3 — persist the drift-kill FLAG only, NOT the history.** The flag (1 bit) captures the time-INVARIANT kill decision (a killed node stays killed across restart). The 4160B DriftHistory (256-sample IC ring) is the detector's time-WINDOWED working memory — `CheckBreach`'s `now−window_us` timestamp filter DISCARDS stale samples on restart → the persisted history is INERT past a window's downtime → persisting it is mostly pointless (corrects the earlier "flag+history=full continuity" — it isn't). Runtime ring UNCHANGED (existing; E.1.2 balloons nothing).
- **D4 — DROP both strategy_id + strategy_state_kind from the wire** (cascade-confirmed both vestigial-on-load: strategy_id cfg-owned `:510`, strategy_state_kind read-and-`(void)`'d `:539`) → ELIMINATES the PERSIST_NO_COMMIT kind entirely (dissolves sweep-finding #4). `resolved_strategy_id` STAYS (committed for display `:505`).
- **D5 — no cache-alignment impact** (all items leave the NodeContext struct unchanged; Phase C = serializer/registry change, not struct-layout). Scope items confirmed: `regime_start_time` `time_t`→`int64_t` (same 8B on target; determinism), `NodeSnap` load-staging generated from the registry (closes a 4th drift surface), `price_samples` typed `FPN_Binary<F>` (type-honest).
- **Hot/slow-path gate (operator: "be extra careful").** Design VERIFIED off the trading paths: the serializer runs on the **Async thread** (`Async.hpp:446/624`, periodic) + **boot** (`Run.hpp:1004`), NOT the hot (`ExecutionCore_Tick`) or per-core slow (rebuild) threads; the NodeContext **struct is unchanged** (hand-declared, D-291) → hot-path reads are byte-identical. MANDATORY Phase-C close-gate (does NOT close until all pass): `calls_graph_diff.sh` CLEAN + `check_latency_path_conformance.py` clean + 3693-suite + the D2 init/reset-identical test.
- **Revised shape (detail → the synthesis doc):** one ordered `FOREACH_NODE_CTX_FIELD` (SCALAR/BIT/PAD/DELEGATE row-kinds, wire-order-faithful) + per-delegate field-by-field registries (regime/feeder + the missing `FOREACH_CONFIDENCE_PERSIST_COUNT`) + walk-completeness; the layout-hash; NO blob delegates; layered determinism gate (frozen committed golden + full-poison distinct fixture + value-round-trip + positive control + wire-length pin + registry-driven population); **TWO-PHASE landing** (byte-identical v10 FIRST → then the v11 delta + SHARDED 10→11 + the version/retire cascade [ledger+tool lockstep + 3 test sites]). AM-4 mid-pair fixture asserts `(bit, partner_pending_pnl)` consistency.
- **Deferred → TECH_DEBT-125:** DriftHistory 256-sample ring → streaming-aggregate (EWMA) / offline-log split for O(1) runtime memory at scale (scale-forward; negligible at 16 nodes, ~17MB at 4096; a detector redesign = behavior change, out of E.1.2's persistence scope).

<!-- D/C/F: D-302 -->
**D-302 — E.1.2 Phase C Step 2 forcing-function architecture: INVERT D-297/D1 — DROP the constexpr layout-hash; PRIMARY = macro-gen emitter + count-locks + frozen golden + golden↔version paired-bump (2026-07-04).** A 9-agent pre-coding I→A cascade (5 i-class ground-truth maps + 4 a-class adversarial refutes; full record `plan_checks/2026-07-04-E.1.2-phaseC-step2-iclass-ground-truth.md`, §A-PASS) REFUTED D-297/D1's *"constexpr persist-layout-hash = PRIMARY forcing function."* A1 (REAL compiles, GCC 16.1.1 + clang 22.1.6, engine flags, identical hash, ~2500× ops margin) killed the feasibility worry BUT proved the framing wrong: the hash catches **no failure class** the trio {macro-generated SAVE/READ/COMMIT emitter (drop-a-wire-op ⟺ drop-a-row, OMS precedent) + per-registry `_PERSIST_COUNT` count-locks + frozen hex-TEXT golden} + A4's **golden↔version paired-bump** (signature in `identifier_ledger.txt` + a paired-bump rule in `check_identifier_retirement.py`, pre-commit Check H → a layout-signature change MECHANICALLY forces the SHARDED_SNAPSHOT_VERSION bump; the D-208/M7 close) doesn't already catch. Its ONLY marginal coverage (count-neutral name-swap at compile-time) is redundant with the golden (test-time) + paired-bump (commit-time); worse, the constexpr hash is **silently weakenable** — a future "tidy" to a concatenated string RED-builds (depth-512), or to a name-blind hash that compiles GREEN and misses every swap = a **Class-51 vacuously-green guard hiding inside the determinism guard** — and taxes every future layout change (hand-maintained opaque `EXPECTED` magic-number; `0xAAAA != 0xBBBB` diagnostics). DECISION (operator, maintainability axis): **Option B — INVERT.** Drop the constexpr hash; the trio + paired-bump is PRIMARY; add a small Python layout-audit tool (sibling to `check_meta_registry.py`) for human-readable layout diffs at the gate. `feedback_guards_compound_enforcement_is_leverage` correctly applied = non-redundant, non-rottable guards (obvious count / tool-regenerated golden / structural ledger coupling), NOT a 4th rottable compile-time guard. Folded cascade constraints (all in ground-truth §A-PASS): **READ dispatches on STORAGE_KIND ONLY** (NO_COMMIT rows still consume bytes → else 2B/node desync); **REC-A** (fold `RecomputeRunningSums` into the confidence delegate `_PersistCommit`) ADOPTED with 4 guardrails (drop only `:588` / KEEP `PortfolioController:2234` / fix the false `ConfidenceScore:1049-52` comment / recompute `dst`); **REC-C** (nested staging) DEFERRED to post-golden (A2+A3: +8B feeder-blob foot-gun + indeterminate-init trap — byte-identity phase stays field-by-field on the existing flat staging); **finalizer stays a post-full-walk block** (never a per-node row; H22 capital-safety); **poison fixture per-FIELD distinct** (all 64 confidence entries, nonzero base — closes the 1440B/node zero desert); MetaRegistry enrollment of the 2 new sub-registries is a FATAL gate.

<!-- D/C/F: D-306 -->
**D-306 — Formal in-code comment/tag SYSTEM (reuse doc-tag-vocab as SSoT, ALL-CAPS code rendering, `[TAG]`/`[DIRECTIVE]`/`[REFERENCE]` axes, deep_dives `====` block anatomy) + DEFER 2d+ trader code to codify-first — 2026-07-05.** Operator-directed (design-depth-is-the-value; `user_deep_design_work_intrinsic_value` + `user_adhd_deferred_reward_discipline`). Emerged from the Check-N `[LAT_EXEMPT]` marker (D-305-adjacent structural fix) → generalized into a full comment scheme for the fox-symdeps in-house dev env. **The scheme:**
- **SSoT vocab reuse:** code tags reuse the existing `DESIGN_SPECS/meta-disciplines/doc-tag-vocabulary.md` (CONCERN + SURFACE axes) — ONE vocabulary across docs AND code; activates the existing sister `categorical-tag-applicability-pattern.md` (the code-level analog, already on the books). CI: extend `check_doc_metadata.py` to validate code tags (typo → fail), same as doc tags.
- **Rendering:** code = ALL-CAPS `UPPER_SNAKE` (`[SLOW_PATH]`); doc frontmatter = lower-hyphen (`slow-path`). Deterministic mapping; cross-grep `rg -i 'slow[-_]path'`. (Operator prefers ALL CAPS in code.)
- **Syntax:** `// [TAG]_[[SLOW_PATH] [WIRE_FORMAT] [PERSISTENCE]]` (surface/concern descriptors, on function-header `====` blocks). Exact bracket nesting OPEN (schema agent).
- **Three axes:** `[TAG]` = surface/concern descriptors · `[DIRECTIVE]` = machine-read (`[LAT_EXEMPT]` = FIRST canonical; future `[H12_PAD]`/`[TODO]`) · `[REFERENCE]` = institutional-memory pointers (`[DESIGN_SPEC]_[..]`/`[MEMORY]_[..]`/`[DECISION]_[D-NNN]`/`[TECH_DEBT]_[TD-NNN]`/`[CLASS]_[NN]`/`[INVARIANT]_[H9]`/`[PLAN]_[..]`) surfaced by the plugin's notes panel = a hyperlinked codebase.
- **Block anatomy:** deep_dives `====` style — WHY-prose (+ optional ASM/math/worked-example blocks, e.g. the branchless hot-path instruction stream) live BETWEEN functions; bodies stay clean. Reconciles the codebase's "inline comments explain WHY" convention → WHY moves to block headers. NOVEL follow-on (captured, not committed): a check that asserts the documented hot-path ASM matches the compiled disassembly (characterization-test for the instruction stream, riding the conformance-analyzer machinery) — makes the comment executable truth.
- **Version-accretion (in-code changelog-as-INDEX, not-a-rewrite):** a function update ADDS a terse `[VERSION]_[id]` block rather than rewriting the header — but as a POINTER, not a copy: `[VERSION]_[..] [DECISION]_[D-NNN] [DATE]_[..]` + a one-line semantic-what. The full WHY stays in the decision log (SSoT); the mechanical diff stays in git (SSoT) — each fact has ONE home, so no drift, no diff-dump bloat. Chains: code → `[VERSION]`+`[DECISION]` → decision-log → rationale; git → bytes. Bounded by plugin-fold (collapse history to a one-line spine) + deep-tail archive to a per-file changelog (sister to `HOT_PATH_CHANGELOG.md`). Formalizes the codebase's existing ad-hoc version tags (`v5.4.0 Phase A.2 …`, the deep_dives `[EDIT [date]]` blocks). Net: the comment system is a navigable INDEX into institutional memory (code=hub, tags=edges, docs=nodes), NOT a duplicate of git/decisions.
- **Documentation-ABOVE-not-sprawled + two-tier (the maintainability keystone):** all WHY-prose/history/diagrams move to a structured block ABOVE the struct/function; the BODY carries only terse inline tags. Sprawl exhibit: `ExecutionCore` (~100 lines of interleaved comments → a ~20-line header + readable body). **Two tiers:** (1) CURATED = judgment, human-written (`[WHY]`/`[TAG]`/`[VERSION]`/`[REFERENCE]`/diagram-intent); (2) **DERIVED = facts, TOOL-written + CI-checked** (`[DATA_SIZE]`/`[DEP_CHAIN]`/`[ALIGNED_CONSUMERS]`/`[THREAD]`/`[SIMD]`/`[DIAGRAM]` byte-map) — auto-generated from `gen_code_map --composition/--byte-context` + `calls_graph_diff` + `check_struct_alignment` (the `sizeof`/`offsetof` pins) + the conformance analyzer, and a build fails if a hand-edit drifts (extends `check_doc_metadata.py` to code). The DERIVED tier is `mechanical-verification-of-derived-code-facts` applied to comments — it is what makes the system SOLVE the stale-comment problem (TECH_DEBT-226/228 class) instead of recreating it. DROPPED as over-complication: embedding commented-out old/new code diffs (duplicates git + drifts + re-bloats) — use the `[VERSION]`+`[DECISION]` pointer instead.
- **`[DIAGRAM]`/`[VISUALIZED]` axis:** ASCII byte-maps (structs: `[active:1][live_tp:24]… = 64B/line0`) + data-flow maps (files: `[TICK]→[SLOW: rolling→regime→rebuild→setparams]→[HOT: BG→SG→TradeEvent]`). ASCII (greppable/diffable/plugin-rendered, not images) → machine-checkable against `offsetof`/`sizeof` (executable truth, like the D-306 ASM-match follow-on). Especially aligned here (codebase #1 principle = layout-by-access-pattern).
- **FINALIZED PARSE-GRAMMAR (the standardized, parse-trivial standard — operator-driven 2026-07-05):** every structured comment line = `// [CATEGORY]…`; the ENTIRE parser is ONE regex `\[([^\[\]]+)\]` (innermost brackets) → token[0]=CATEGORY (closed ALL-CAPS set), rest=values; the outer `[[..]]` list-grouping is visual-only (non-innermost → skipped), so `[TAG]_[[SLOW_PATH] [ML]]`→`[TAG,SLOW_PATH,ML]` free. NO per-category regex, NO naming-convention parsing. Common retrieval = plain substring grep (ALL-CAPS bracketed tokens never collide with prose → precise, no-overlap, "find only what you need"). `====`(major)/`----`(minor) bars = plugin FOLD boundaries (render, NOT parse). **CATEGORY set:** `[FILE]`/`[STRUCT]`/`[FUNCTION]` · `[TAG]` · `[WHY]` · `[DIAGRAM]` · `[DERIVED]`{`[DATA_SIZE]` `[DEP_CHAIN]` `[ALIGNED_CONSUMERS]` `[THREAD]` `[SIMD]`} · `[VERSION]` · `[REFERENCE]`{`[DESIGN_SPEC]` `[MEMORY]` `[DECISION]` `[TECH_DEBT]` `[CLASS]` `[INVARIANT]` `[PLAN]` `[AUDIT]`} · `[DIRECTIVE]`{`[LAT_EXEMPT]`…} · `[SCHEMA]_[vN]` (self-versions the convention → migratable) · `[END_STRUCT]`/`[END_FUNCTION]` closers (plugin fold-range + sub-file jump-nav → uncaps file size, consistent with the AI-workflow file-size relaxation). `[DEPRECATED_INFORMATION]` = a redirect-INDEX changelog (what moved where + why; git holds the removed content).
- **Standardized ACCESS/FIND pattern (the capstone):** (a) a CANONICAL SECTION ORDER per block = the "access ladder" — `[FILE]/[STRUCT]/[FUNCTION]` (WHAT) → `[TAG]` (CLASSIFY) → `[WHY]`/`[DIAGRAM]` (UNDERSTAND) → `[DERIVED]` (FACTS) → `[VERSION]` (WHEN) → `[REFERENCE]` (DRILL-OUT); CI-ENFORCEABLE (out-of-order block fails) → consistency guaranteed. (b) a COMPOSABLE multi-axis drill-down — the orthogonal axes (unit / classify / when / where) compose in any order via `rg -l <axis1> | xargs rg -l <axis2> | …`, the operator's `[TAG]→[VERSION/DATE]→[REFERENCE]` sequence — which is the SAME `rg|xargs rg` recipe `doc-tag-vocabulary.md § Retrieval recipes` already documents → ONE retrieval grammar across docs AND code. Net standard = ONE parse rule (innermost brackets) + ONE section order (CI-enforced) + ONE compose recipe (orthogonal axes). **Design considered COMPLETE for codification 2026-07-05** — schema-agent writes the full spec (grammar + ladder + axes + two-tier DERIVED + diagrams + CI teeth + the ExecutionCore & Regime_Classify worked examples); orchestrator lands the Check-N `[LAT_EXEMPT]` first-application (unblocks 2b+2c).
- **Retrieval-index + final-round tags (2026-07-05):** the standardized format enables an INVERTED INDEX (`{category:{value:[loc]}}`) → queries = posting-list intersections (O(matches); the operator's "by-layers" access — store tag-1's lines, intersect tag-2's); the plugin builds+caches it (ripgrep is already ms-fast → the index is the INTERACTIVE/composed-query win, NOT a correctness need). NEW tags: `[ITERATIONS]` (DERIVED, git-churn count → hot-spot detector) · `[FUTURE_WORK]_[TECH_DEBT|PLAN]_[id]` (code twin of the `future-expansion` doc-concept; a tracked ledger POINTER, not a floating note) · `[REFERENCE]`-pointer CI-VALIDATION (the linked decision/spec/memory MUST resolve — `check_capture_audit`-sentinel shape → no dangling refs; plugin notes-panel opens it inline = bidirectional code↔workspace). "Matches-current-spec" = `[SCHEMA]_[vN]` (comment-format currency) + the referenced spec's OWN CI (conformance) — NOT a hand-maintained bool (drift). Spec deliverable includes a BLANK fill-in template + examples (code-level `/doc-create`).
- **GATE RESOLVED 2026-07-05:** operator confirmed the engine repo is **PRIVATE now** → workspace-refs in source are leak-safe → the code↔workspace reference-tie-in is **GREENLIT**. Go-forward policy = engine + workspace treated as a UNIT (both-private or both-public *together*), NOT the asymmetric code-public / apparatus-hidden split. **IN-FLUX (operator's pending deliberate call):** the eventual public-vs-private direction — the public AGPL repo was the hedge-fund-visibility surface (career-load-bearing per `user_public_work_attracts_hedge_funds`); orchestrator flagged the trade-off once, non-blocking. `project_public_repo_is_code_only` memory = **flagged-for-reconcile**, NOT rewritten on the interim "private for now" (operator to lock the go-forward direction deliberately; reconcile the memory then).
- **OPERATING STANCE (settled 2026-07-05): ASSUME-ALL-VISIBLE — don't hide.** Regardless of eventual public/private, the comment system operates as-if-everything-is-visible + ties `[DECISION]`/`[DESIGN_SPEC]`/`[MEMORY]` refs freely (the hiding was the ONLY complexity; dropping it is simpler + more usable — operator's call). The eventual repo-visibility decision stays SEPARATE + deliberate (career angle noted once, non-blocking). So: build the system for maximum usability now; the public/private surface is a later, independent lever, not a design constraint on the comment convention.
- **DEFER DECISION (2026-07-05):** once schema **v1.0 + a fill-in template** exist, DEFER all 2d+ persist code until the codebase is CONVERTED to the format (operator: it's an organizational change that makes navigation easier, "genuinely warranted"). **Orchestrator honest sequencing guidance (accepted):** DERIVED-tooling FIRST — hand-converting before the size/dep/index generator exists would hand-write the very facts that drift (recreating the problem). Order = schema v1.0 → DERIVED/CI generator → **tool-assisted INCREMENTAL** conversion (pilot a few files → validate the format in real use → roll out; NOT a monolithic hand-freeze) → new code uses the format immediately. **CRITICAL PATH = schema v1.0** (unblocks conversion + plugin + the deferral). **C++ nav-tool right-sized:** the win is STRUCTURED parsing + a cached INVERTED-INDEX (NOT "faster than grep" — ripgrep is already SIMD/parallel/elite); the grammar is parse-trivial (one innermost-bracket regex) → a shared parser is nice-to-have + C++/pybind11 is PREMATURE (build the C++ indexer ONLY if the plugin needs sub-ms interactive at real scale); the `[CODE]`/`[END_*]` code-range delimiters ARE valuable (the DERIVED size-tool extracts the body to measure it). A shared `CORE_TOOLS_HELPER` parser (SSoT for the grammar; Python-bound for CI tools) is reasonable but not urgent given the trivial grammar.
- **Ownership split (parallel):** the operator's SCHEMA AGENT owns the style-ref DOC + vocab extensions + `====` anatomy (DOCS ONLY — no `.hpp`/`.py`); the orchestrator owns the decision-log + the Check-N structural fix (the first `[LAT_EXEMPT]` application). Shared contract = the literal token `[LAT_EXEMPT]`.
- **SEQUENCE decision:** DEFER 2d+ persist code; codify the schema FIRST so it self-propagates ("you copy the existing style"). The Check-N fix is NOT deferred — it is the FIRST schema application + it UNBLOCKS the verified-but-uncommitted 2b+2c (regime+feeder delegates). Order: capture (this entry) → schema-doc (agent) → Check-N fix (first `[LAT_EXEMPT]`) → commit 2b+2c clean → 2d+ resumes with the new style.
- **Latency-neutrality of 2b VERIFIED (operator asked):** the compiled instruction STREAM of Regime_Classify is byte-identical clean-vs-2b (every branch addr/opcode, `float=18`, `div=0`, `spills=0`, `indirect=0`); the Check-N block is PURELY the FILE:LINE→marker exemption fragility (a pre-existing cold-debug fprintf shifted `:642`→`:708`), NOT a real latency regression. Sister anti-pattern to codify: **false-RED guard** (a guard keyed by a fragile locator that fires on a benign edit → trains the SKIP reflex → rots into Class-51 vacuously-green; the INVERSE + upstream cause of Class-51).

<!-- D/C/F: D-307 -->
**D-307 — In-code comment SYSTEM reframe: the PLUGIN owns the machine layer (comments NEVER mirror clangd-derived facts); comments = CURATED human layer only + the schema↔plugin parallel-work contract — 2026-07-05.** AMENDS D-306's two-tier keystone, after reading `fox-symdeps.nvim` (operator is building the plugin in a parallel session). **The plugin already derives the ENTIRE `[DERIVED]` tier LIVE from ground truth** (clangd + treesitter + `gen_code_map`): size/alignment/cache-line/vector-reg, per-field layout, upstream `Uses`, role-classified `Consumers`, call directions, hot-path instruction budget, doc-mentions, byte-layout blast-radius. A DERIVED *comment* tier would MIRROR what the plugin shows live = the **Class-18 anti-pattern the whole system exists to kill** (operator's own "was the old way better since it parses code anyway?" caught it). **DECISION:** comments NEVER carry derived facts. **Two tiers REFRAMED:** (1) CURATED = the comment block (`[WHY]`/`[DETAIL]`/`[TAG]`/`[EDIT]`/`[REFERENCE]`(+`[DOC]_[path]` sync)/`[DIAGRAM]`) — the human layer the compiler can't know; (2) DERIVED = the plugin's LIVE overlay (clangd), NOT comments; the HUD FUSES both (curated block + live facts = the polished GUI). **What it buys:** the drift surface DISAPPEARS — no DERIVED-regenerator, no DERIVED-CI-drift-check; the stale-comment class (TD-226/228) is solved by having NO mirror (the fact lives once, at ground truth). **Schema↔plugin CONTRACT (the parallel-work seam):** the comment block IS the interface; stable contract = {closed category vocab · section ladder · one-category-per-line · the `====` bracket structure}; changes WITHIN it (richer prose, examples) are free + independent, changes TO it (a new category / ladder slot) are the ONLY coordination point → gated by a `[SCHEMA]_[vN]` bump. H22-clean seam: each side a pure function of the interface; the (N+1)th plugin feature needs zero schema change. Orchestrator owns the schema/format; operator owns the plugin/consumer. **Style CONFIRMED by `deep_dives/libraries/ML_Headers/LinearRegression3X.hpp`:** all-`====` (DROP `----` — operator's native style), section-bracketed headers, `[EDIT [date]]` version blocks (operator already invented `[VERSION]`), rich first-person narrative `[WHY]`/`[DETAIL]` (the LR `_Fit` covariance walk-through IS the target — preserve voice, a HARD rule). Folds the prior line-rule refinements: **one-category-per-line** (parse-critical — `token[0]=category` requires it, so `[REFERENCE]`+`[INVARIANT]` are SEPARATE lines) + **long lists = block form** (`[CONSUMERS]` header → indented `//   [X]` value-lines; inline only when short). Spec = `DESIGN_SPECS/doc-disciplines/in-code-documentation-schema.md` (v1.0, stage 2-draft, **co-evolving with the plugin — NOT locked**; open edge-cases: overloads → sig-discriminated identity; build-config-dependent facts → pinned to canonical `build.sh`; exhaustive example-sweep to one-per-line + all-`====`).
**D-307 AMENDMENT (same-day — operator feedback on the first rendered examples):** (1) **deep_dives is a HISTORICAL/style reference ONLY — do NOT link it, do NOT canonize it** (early sketch; operator wants MORE POLISHED). Carried forward: the `[EDIT [date]]` idea + hand-typed ASCII diagrams + rich first-person voice. REVISED: operator prefers the **GROUPED** block (named `[META]`/`[DOC]`/`[DERIVED]`/`[REFS]` groups; `====` between groups + `----` under the group-name) over the flat all-`====` sketch. (2) **Diagrams = hand-typed ASCII** (`-> <- | - _ ^ v`), NEVER Unicode box/arrow glyphs. (3) **`[DERIVED]`-drop REOPENED as an OPEN FORK:** (a) plugin-live-only vs (b) a TOOL-REFRESHED in-comment summary (generator-written + CI-checked = a materialized ground-truth snapshot, NOT hand-drift — the Class-18 concern only bites HAND mirrors). Operator's Version-A preference + *"comments drift slower than docs"* → orchestrator leans (b); operator's call. (4) **`[DOC]_[path]` sync DROPPED** — docs drift faster than code → rich `[DETAIL]` lives IN the comment, next to the code. (5) Misc: `[DEP_CHAIN]_in` → `[UPSTREAM]` + `[CONSUMERS]`; per-function changelog = UNNEEDED (git holds removed text + `[EDIT]` holds semantic history + decision-log holds why); `[DOC_TAG]`/`[CODE_TAG]` vocab-split = REJECTED (ONE shared SSoT vocab; file-type disambiguates) BUT two indexes (code-index + doc-index from the one vocab) = useful.
**D-307 FORKS RESOLVED 2026-07-05 (operator: "get this done today, better organized"):** **DERIVED = (b) tool-refreshed in-comment** (operator leaned b; reversible if the plugin makes it redundant). **Block structure = the `[COMMENT]` dated-partition form:** `[[DATE] [VERSION]]` partitions (newest-first, `——` em-dash separated), each with its comment + a `[SUPPORTING_DOCS]` sub-block (per-version provenance) — unifies why+history+refs into ONE accreting stream (newest partition = current WHY; older = history); sits alongside `[META]` + `[DIAGRAM]` sections; the code BODY is pristine (comments moved OUT). **Bars = 3-tier: `====` major / `——` em-dash middle (between partitions) / `----` minor — RENDER-ONLY, NOT parsed → cosmetic + find-replace-swappable anytime.** **Per-function changelog RE-ENDORSED** (my earlier "unneeded ceremony" was WRONG — a dead-comment graveyard is still git's job, but an annotated per-version evolution + provenance is institutional memory, = the `[EDIT]` accretion richer) — BOUNDED by plugin-fold (latest shown, old collapsed) + append-only deep-tail per-file archive (frozen past = no drift). **`[DOC]_[path]` sync DROPPED; `[DEP_CHAIN]_in`→`[UPSTREAM]`+`[CONSUMERS]`; ONE shared vocab (no DOC/CODE fork) + two indexes.** Spec v1.0 STRUCTURALLY LOCKED; exhaustive example-sweep to the `[COMMENT]`-form = remaining P1-adjacent polish (tracked in E.1.2.A).

<!-- D/C/F: D-308 -->
**D-308 — E.1.2.A schema v1 completion (this-session spec edits, applying D-306/307) — 2026-07-05.** Reconciled fork-b + completed the spec (commit `c07a6c0` + staged edits): (1) fork-b applied to the STALE narrative — §"Two tiers" + category table + "Explicitly NOT done" still described fork-a ("comments never carry derived facts / no CI-drift-check") → reconciled to "DERIVED tool-refreshed in-comment + CI-checked; Class-18 only bites HAND mirrors." (2) `[DOC]`-sync DROPPED (D-307 straggler). (3) FOLDED the designed-but-unlocked D-306 tags `[ITERATIONS]` (git-churn DERIVED) + `[FUTURE_WORK]` (ledger forward-pointer). (4) `[CODE]`/`[END_CODE]` body-range delimiter (`====`-framed; code at [CODE]+2) — bars are render-only so a PARSED delimiter is needed for the plugin's cursor-position tracking + the size-tool. (5) struct DERIVED += `[ALIGN]`/`[CACHE_LINES]`/`[STRADDLE]` (cache/L1d/false-sharing, H6). (6) function DERIVED += `[BRANCHES]` (H7/H20) + `[FLOAT]` (H4) + `[BUILD]` (canonical build.sh flags pin). (7) `[SCOPE]_[DEPLOYMENT|CLUSTER|NODE|CORE]` (H22; `[TYPE]` was taken by typedefs). (8) `[FROZEN]`/`[GOLDEN]` byte-identity guardrail + `[REFERENCE]_[GOLDEN]`. (9) structural/concurrency: `[BIT_PACKED]`+bit-map, `[SYNC]_[SEQ_LOCK|SPSC|ATOMIC|LOCK_FREE]`, `[PADDING]`, `[WIRE_FORMAT]`/`[PERSISTED]`. (10) `[THREAD]` DERIVED→CURATED (author-declared, not clang-derivable — was a Class-51 vacuously-green hole in the auto-checked tier). (11) preserve-voice CARVE-OUT: DERIVED-class facts (byte-maps/sizes/versions/row-shapes) are TOOL-REGENERATED never relocated; only rationale/history/voice preserved verbatim. (12) `[LAT_EXEMPT]`-unit-level for non-latency code. **Reproducibility rule (operator-driven):** DERIVED must be deterministic-per-binary → **instruction-count** (pinned by `[BUILD]` flags + `[INSTANTIATION]` template-args) NOT measured-ns (machine-variable — clock/turbo/voltage/interrupts; operator observed hot p99 76ns–1.1µs/256-tick). H8 ns budget = a `[REFERENCE]_[INVARIANT]_[H8]` design constraint; ns lives on the separate measurement surfaces (fox-bench / data+monitoring planes), never in-comment.

<!-- D/C/F: D-319 -->
**D-319 — Plugin gains a BOUNDED write-role (curated edit-assist / DERIVED refresh-only / scaffold-generate; CI validates) — 2026-07-05.** Operator asked whether the plugin should edit/add/update tags. Answer splits along the CURATED-vs-DERIVED line (the two-tier discipline): (1) **CURATED** (`[TAG]`/`[REFERENCE]`/`[WHY]`/`[OVERVIEW]`/`[DIAGRAM]`) → YES, edit-assist: vocab-completion for `[TAG]`, live resolve-check for `[REFERENCE]` (the resolver's Lua twin), `[SUPPORTING_DOCS]` insert. (2) **Scaffold a whole block** → YES — this IS the scaffold generator (spec's "single highest-leverage tool after the CI validator"; already says it fits fox-symdeps as an editor command). (3) **Refresh DERIVED** → YES but REGENERATE-only (from ground truth). (4) **Hand-edit a DERIVED value** → NO — the exact Class-18 drift the system exists to kill; DERIVED is visually read-only, regenerate-never-free-type. **Safety constraint:** writers go through the SAME grammar + SAME fact-producers as the CI validator (unified-fact-grammar contract #14) → a plugin-written block is CI-green by construction, and plugin-written DERIVED can't disagree with CI-expected DERIVED (single-source the COMPUTATION, not two impls). Plugin writes, CI verifies — same grammar both sides. Closes the loop with the completeness detector: CI finds "missing `[BRANCHES]`", a plugin "refresh/fill" command adds it. NOT super-critical / no adjudication (operator flagged the over-consult) — FORCED by Class-18 + the locked two-tier discipline; captured as a settled refinement of the schema's "Division of labor" section, not an open decision.
<!-- STATUS: DECIDED (architecture, forced by existing discipline) — plugin write-role scoped; build-order is operator's (plugin domain). One line folded into schema § Division-of-labor. -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-320 -->
**D-320 — Cache-layout CI gate: auto-calculate layout → gate on break → refresh tags → trace the reason; NEVER auto-align — 2026-07-05 (operator direction).** Operator: "auto calculate the structs, make sure they dont break, warn/fail CI if something breaks alignment, then the associated tag updates, and shows the exact reason it broke by running a trace using the tools we have." Design: (1) **DETECT** — real per-field offsets/size/align + cache-line straddlers from the ground-truth layout dump. (2) **GATE** — a straddler on a cross-thread struct (`[THREAD]`/`alignas(64)` signal) = false-sharing → FAIL (H6); a size drift vs the `[SIZE]` tag / `static_assert` size-pin → FAIL. **The tag system makes detection CONTEXT-AWARE (the payoff):** `[THREAD]`+`[STRADDLE]` together answer "does this straddle MATTER" — cross-thread straddle = bug (RED), cold single-thread straddle = benign (note). Blind straddle-detection flags everything; tag-aware is a precise H6 gate. (3) **REFRESH** the `[SIZE]`/`[ALIGN]`/`[CACHE_LINES]`/`[STRADDLE]` DERIVED tags from truth (the D-319 refresh-writer). (4) **TRACE** on break — exact field + offset + which 64B line it crosses + the `[THREAD]` context. **NEVER auto-align** (operator explicit): reordering/padding a serialized/persisted struct silently breaks wire+snapshot compat (H21/H12, Knight-adjacent) → detect+flag+SUGGEST, human approves. **SSoT architecture (locked):** reuse the plugin's `recordlayout.lua`/`bytemap.lua` (`M.straddlers()` `:122`/`:135` ALREADY computes straddlers, tested) as the layout computer — do NOT reimplement `-fdump-record-layouts` parsing in Python (the Class-18 mirror the system bans). One producer, two consumers (HUD + CI). ~80% already built (plugin computes straddles live — the `s` lens; `check_struct_alignment.py` has the alignas/size-pin guards); remaining = glue (materialize-into-tags + the DERIVED-vs-truth gate). Foundation proven: clang++ `-fdump-record-layouts` on `main.cpp` → 555 real record layouts.

<!-- D/C/F: D-321 -->
**D-321 — DERIVED-fact toolchain split SHARPENED: LAYOUT via clang (ABI-identical), CODEGEN via g++ ONLY (clang≠g++ 1:1) — 2026-07-05 (operator).** Sharpens D-310/D-313. The two fact-families do NOT share a toolchain: (1) **LAYOUT facts** (`[SIZE]_[bytes]`/`[ALIGN]`/`[CACHE_LINES]`/`[STRADDLE]`/offsets) → clang `-Xclang -fdump-record-layouts` is CORRECT + canonical (clang-only flag; Itanium ABI → byte-identical to the shipped g++ for the engine's POD/H12/H14-no-bitfield structs). (2) **CODEGEN facts** (`[SIZE]_[instr]`/`[BRANCHES]`/`[SIMD]`/spills) → **MUST be g++**, the shipped compiler — clang's instruction selection + branch codegen DIFFER from g++ 1:1, so a clang instr-count / branch-classification is WRONG for the binary we actually run. `[BUILD]` pins the canonical g++ `build.sh` flags for the codegen facts (D-312: concrete `x86-64-v3`, never `-march=native`). Plugin already respects this (asmexplorer/branchtag = g++; layout = clang — D-313); this LOCKS it as a schema/DERIVED-check constraint so a later session doesn't compute instr/branch with clang. **Operational sub-finding (operator heads-up):** `compile_commands.json` is g++-oriented (`-flto`/`-funroll-loops` + g++ diagnostics) → the clang LAYOUT probe needs the portable-flag subset (`-I`/`-isystem`/`-D`/`-std`; exactly `sizeprobe._filter_flags`). This flag-filter is a SHARED HUD+CI dependency — reuse the plugin's, don't fork it.
<!-- STATUS: DECIDED (constraint) — clang=LAYOUT / g++=CODEGEN locked; compile_commands clang-cleanliness = a tracked shared dependency (TECH_DEBT filed). -->

**D-323 — OPEN ARCHITECTURE QUESTION (operator-raised, CRITICAL): node/cluster CORE-TOPOLOGY + mediation-tier scaling — does a node need a 3rd (mediator) core, and how does the producer/drainer/controller tier scale? — 2026-07-05.** Raised during the E.1.2.A cache-residency discussion (TD-232). NOT a decision — a homed investigation CHARTER for post-info-infra → the rest-of-E. **Current topology:** a node = 2 CPUs (HOT thread + SLOW thread); slow→hot mediated by a SEQLOCK (lock-free, in-band — NO mediator); producer→hot + hot→drainer by SPSC rings; a SINGLE GLOBAL producer (fan-out to N tick-rings) + SINGLE GLOBAL drainer (drain N event-rings → OMS) do the cross-node mediation. ⇒ for CORRECTNESS a per-node 3rd core is NOT needed (lock-free primitives + shared globals already offload mediation off the hot/slow threads). **The real questions are SCALING + ISOLATION:** (1) hot↔slow thread → PHYSICAL-core mapping (separate cores vs SMT siblings → L1d-eviction contention, TD-232); (2) do the SINGLE global producer/drainer become the bottleneck/contention point at high N (16 nodes) or multi-cluster (.E.1) → must they SHARD (per-cluster drainers / a mediation tier)?; (3) cluster-controller core allocation + node↔controller comms; (4) cross-cluster coordination (the Σ exposure-cap aggregator, E.5/TD-211) — dedicated mediation?; (5) would a dedicated per-node/per-cluster MEDIATOR core reduce hot-path JITTER (keep the hot thread PURE) + parallelize mediation at scale (the throughput-headroom framing, D-322-adjacent)? **Operator frame:** capture now (CRITICAL), finish the info-infra (E.1.2.A) first, THEN revisit how this fits the E-series (horizontal-scale vision H22 + multi-exchange .E.1). Homed: TECH_DEBT-233. Sisters: TD-232 (cache residency), H22 (scale-invariance).
<!-- STATUS: OPEN-INVESTIGATION (CRITICAL) — deferred to post-E.1.2.A → rest-of-E topology review; NOT decided. Homed TD-233. -->

<!-- D/C/F: D-324 -->
**D-324 — OPEN SECURITY/RESILIENCE QUESTION (operator-raised): adversarial BURST-OVERLOAD of the data pipeline (quote-stuffing / burst sell-offs) + countermeasures — 2026-07-05.** Continuation of the D-322 rate/headroom + TD-232/233 hot-path-under-load thread. **Attack surface (understand-to-DEFEND):** a competitor can weaponize a market-data BURST — a rapid sequence of aggressive orders/quotes/cancels (quote-stuffing) or a burst sell-off — to spike the market-data rate EVERY participant's feed handler must process. If your INBOUND pipeline can't hold ρ<1 through the burst, your `tick_ring` backs up → tail latency → STALE data → you react late while the attacker (who induced the noise + filters it) gains the edge. Direct vector = INBOUND (the market-data feed → your tick_ring/parser), NOT your outbound (internal); outbound stress is a secondary cascade (a burst that trips many of your gates fills `event_ring`/OMS). **Countermeasures (defensive hardening):** (1) HEADROOM — process far faster than peak burst rate so ρ<1 even under a burst (primary; ties D-322); (2) SKIP-TO-LATEST / coalescing — during a burst the HOT trading path processes the FRESHEST tick (a stale queued tick is worthless for a decision), bounding queue latency. **⚠ TENSION (operator insight): skip-to-latest must NOT naively drop for the SLOW path** — regime / rolling / flow stats need the FULL data stream, so the dropped ticks' statistical contribution is COALESCED / aggregated (volume / min / max / count / VWAP), NOT lost; AND a RAW burst SKEWS the regime (a stuffing burst is noise, not signal), so burst-noise is FILTERED (per (3)) rather than ingested. ⇒ the two consumers have DIFFERENT completeness needs: **skip-to-latest for the DECISION, coalesce + filter for the STATS** — a shared naive skip-to-latest would starve the slow path; (3) burst / quote-stuffing DETECTION + filtering — recognize abnormal message-rate signatures + deprioritize the noise (don't treat every stuffed quote as signal); (4) feed-handler RESILIENCE (the branchless / SIMD parser, H5) + a tick_ring sized for transient bursts; (5) hot-path ISOLATION so it keeps its speed under load (TD-232); (6) burst-as-SIGNAL — a burst sell-off IS information (someone's dumping) → detect + trade the volatility (the regime detector's VOLATILE path), turning the attack into an opportunity; (7) graceful DEGRADATION under overload (skip-to-latest / widen / reduce), never unbounded lag. **Candidate tag-axis:** a `[BURST_SENSITIVE]` / `[BACKPRESSURE]` classify value marking the burst-overload-exposed paths (feed parser / tick_ring / drain) — pairs with the `[HOT_WORKING_SET]` axis (TD-232), fold-when-piloted. **Dual-use note:** DEPLOYING this offensively (deliberately stuffing to degrade competitors) is quote-stuffing = MARKET MANIPULATION (SEC/regulator-pursued) — so the frame is DEFENSIVE hardening + burst-as-signal, NOT an attack to run. **Operator frame:** capture now, design countermeasures in the rest-of-E / pre-live hardening. Homed: TECH_DEBT-234. Sisters: D-322 (headroom), TD-232 (residency), TD-233 (topology).
<!-- STATUS: OPEN-INVESTIGATION — deferred to rest-of-E / pre-live hardening; countermeasures TBD. Homed TD-234. Frame = DEFENSIVE (offensive deployment = manipulation, out of scope). -->

Plugin reads both; its `tagadapter` must target the CODIFIED grammar (`[SIZE]`/`[UPSTREAM]`/`[COMMENT]`/`[CODE]`), NOT the pre-fork example in `TAG-INTEGRATION.md`. **Propagation sweep (D-322 reconcile gaps):** FIXED now — schema vocab-note un-staled + the 4 reuse-mappings embedded (author-discoverable). HOMED backlog (plan § Propagation + 3-surface alignment): TAG_INDEX regen · BUILD the code-tag index · SUBAGENT_ARMING (0 tag-refs → add the grammar) · align plugin `TAG-INTEGRATION.md` (operator-owned) · verify CLAUDE.md / categorical-tag-applicability / doc-frontmatter-convention + the 4 tag-skills. **The auto-propagation tool = the highest-leverage #14 build** (framework-discipline: H15-style auto-flow > manual sync, on the tag layer). Plugin-vision (`TAG-INTEGRATION.md`) confirmed ALIGNED with the built split: plugin = editor-side GENERATOR + drift-VERIFIER of `[DERIVED]`, CI (validator + the D-320 cache-gate) = CI-side verifier, reading the SAME facts.

**D-326 — Inline field comments + sub-group headers STAY in `[CODE]` (verbatim); only unit-level WHY relocates to `[COMMENT]` — 2026-07-06 (operator, from the `RegimeSignals` screenshot).** Operator: *"something like this probably benefits from the inline comments though … following this new tag system."* A densely field-documented struct (`RegimeSignals` — 40+ signal fields, each with a `// relative price slope`-style annotation, grouped by `// short window (128-tick)` sub-headers) must NOT have its per-field docs stripped into `[COMMENT]` — that destroys the field↔doc locality that makes it readable. **Codified:** conversion relocates ONLY the unit-level WHY prose (what the whole unit is + why) to `[COMMENT]`; existing per-field inline comments + sub-group section headers are field-LOCAL documentation → stay inside `[CODE]`, verbatim. Discriminator: **explains-the-whole-unit → `[COMMENT]`; explains-one-field/sub-group → stays inline.** (Reconciles with the pre-existing "fields get terse inline tags, never prose" line — that governs ADDING tags; this governs PRESERVING existing field docs.) Landed: schema § Granularity.
<!-- STATUS: DECIDED + LANDED (schema § Coverage/Granularity) — 2026-07-06. -->

**D-327 — `[DERIVED]` splits WRITTEN (stable) vs LIVE-PREVIEW (volatile); the write-in-place generator SHIPPED — 2026-07-06 (operator: "it doesn't output to the actual comment place, which sucks" + the `[SIZE]_[0 instr]` observation).** Two findings fused. (1) **The `0 instr`** on `CumDelta_Init<F>` = an un-instantiated template has nothing to count → instruction-count (like `[SIMD]`/`[BRANCHES]`) is compiler-flag-volatile + instantiation-dependent. Writing it makes the committed source a lie on the next `-O`/`-march` change → CI churn for zero signal. **Codified split:** WRITTEN `[DERIVED]` = STABLE facts only — struct layout (`[SIZE]`-bytes/`[ALIGN]`/`[STRADDLE]`/`[CACHE_LINES]`, ABI-fixed → `check_cache_layout.py --fix`) + call-graph (`[UPSTREAM]`/`[CONSUMERS]`, source-fixed → plugin). Volatile (instr/simd/branches) = LIVE-PREVIEW-only (`:FoxSymdepsDerived` preview + HUD asm — *why* it asks you to instantiate), NEVER written. (2) **Preview-only was backwards** — a HUD that computes truth but makes you hand-copy it in. **SHIPPED the generator:** `tagwriter.lua` (write the stable facts into the `[DERIVED]` block, MERGING — keeps the cache-gate's `[SIZE]`/`[ALIGN]`, drops volatile placeholders + old call-graph) + `:FoxSymdepsDerived!` bang (write) vs plain (preview) + `tagcontext.lua` (resolve the unit from the ENCLOSING block, so it fires with the cursor ANYWHERE in the unit, not just on the symbol). Per-unit-type writer: STRUCT → `--fix`; FUNCTION → `:FoxSymdepsDerived!`. **Zero hand-written derived facts** (anti-Class-18). Landed: schema § Two tiers + plugin (`tag_grammar_adapter`/`tagwriter`/`tagcontext`, tag-layer uncommitted pending operator sign-off). Sisters: D-307 (DERIVED tool-refreshed), D-319 (plugin bounded WRITE-role), D-320 (cache-gate), D-321 (layout=clang / codegen=g++).
<!-- STATUS: DECIDED + LANDED (schema + plugin generator built + tested; tag-layer uncommitted pending sign-off) — 2026-07-06. -->

**D-328 — Context-aware action palette (`:FoxSymdepsMenu` / `<leader>dm`): the tag `[TYPE]` filters which ops are offered — 2026-07-06 (operator-envisioned).** Operator: *"a 'run hotkeys' that brings up a list, then lets you select appropriate things based on what can be used for each thing within a function/struct/registry … since we have the tag system, it's easy right?"* — correct, and here's why: the genuinely hard part (which unit is the cursor in · what TYPE) is already solved by `tagcontext.enclosing_block` (`{type,name}` from anywhere in a block), and the ops already exist as the 14 `<leader>d*` functions. So the palette is a THIN router: resolve type → filter → `vim.ui.select` → run. **Framework-shaped (the elegant part):** an action registry (`actions.lua`) where each op = ONE row `{label, applies_to, run}` — the codebase's X-macro-registry discipline applied to the editor UX (add a capability = add a row). Filtered menus (tested): FUNCTION → {HUD · panel · derived preview/write · asm explorer · branch-tags}; STRUCT → {HUD · panel · derived · cache-straddle · ambient-layout · lock-layout static_assert}; REGISTRY / not-in-a-block → universal only. Consolidates 14 memorize-me keybinds into ONE discoverable, context-correct entry — a direct payoff of the machine-readable `[TYPE]`. Also landed this pass: `:FoxSymdepsDerived!` now AUTO-SAVES (persist so disk==buffer; safe — `.clang-format` is DisableFormat). Sisters: D-327 (the write it routes to), `tagcontext` (the resolver), the X-macro-registry-with-presence-dispatch pattern (the shape).

**D-329 — D-326 generalized to function bodies + `[SECTION]_[<label>]` within-function markers — 2026-07-06 (operator, from converting `Regime_ComputeSignals`).** Two linked refinements. **(1) D-326 GENERALIZED** — "inline comments stay" was worded for struct *fields*; it extends to **function-body step-comments** (`// relative slope: normalize by price`, `// variance ratio…`). ALL code-LOCAL comments (struct fields · function step-comments · sub-group headers) stay in `[CODE]` verbatim; ONLY the unit-level WHY relocates to `[COMMENT]`. The `[CODE]` body is only ever *wrapped*, never rewritten. Caught + fixed **two leaked "comments moved OUT" framings** (the function template line 186 `<clean body — comments moved OUT>` + SUBAGENT_ARMING § 2.6) that flat-out contradicted D-326 — the sister-cohort miss. **(2) `[SECTION]_[<label>]` — NEW category** (curated · code-local · OPTIONAL): marks a logical phase inside a big multi-part function's body (`// [SECTION]_[short window signals]`) — the machine-readable form of the operator's "demarcation points" idea. It *replaces* the plain prose section-comment with the same text made navigable (label = the value; spaces OK); the plugin reads them as a **within-function TOC / jump-list**. Use for fns with ≥3 phases (`Regime_ComputeSignals`); a small fn needs none; a one-line computation keeps its plain comment. Landed: `category-set` fence + structural-annotations note + function template. Operator: *"tags within each function to mark where each purpose of it is."* Candidate plugin feature: `[SECTION]` jump-list (sister to the HUD's section nav). Sisters: D-326 (code-local stays), D-322 (vocab extensibility).

The convergence of E.1.2.A (tag system) + the plugin-nav vision + the decoupling endgoal (`plans/_future/2026-05-12-decoupling-endgoal-roadmap.md` — viewers attach to the runtime).
